#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>
#include <iostream>
#include <climits>

using namespace std;

struct TurnData {
    bool possible[4];
    long long sum_d[4];
    long long max_d[4];
};

vector<vector<int>> s_table(4, vector<int>(10, 0));

void precompute_s_table() {
    // m=0: all 0
    for (int pm = 0; pm < 10; ++pm)
        s_table[0][pm] = 0;

    // m=1
    for (int pm = 0; pm < 10; ++pm)
        s_table[1][pm] = ((pm + 1) % 10 == 0) ? 1 : 0;

    // m=2
    for (int pm = 0; pm < 10; ++pm) {
        bool found = false;
        for (int j = 1; j <= 2; ++j)
            if ((pm + j) % 10 == 0) {
                found = true;
                break;
            }
        s_table[2][pm] = found ? 1 : 0;
    }

    // m=3
    for (int pm = 0; pm < 10; ++pm) {
        bool found = false;
        for (int j = 1; j <= 3; ++j)
            if ((pm + j) % 10 == 0) {
                found = true;
                break;
            }
        s_table[3][pm] = found ? 1 : 0;
    }
}

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    precompute_s_table();

    int n;
    cin >> n;

    vector<TurnData> turns;

    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        int k;
        cin >> k;
        vector<int> cost1, cost2, cost3;

        for (int j = 0; j < k; ++j) {
            int c, d;
            cin >> c >> d;
            if (c == 1) cost1.push_back(d);
            else if (c == 2) cost2.push_back(d);
            else cost3.push_back(d);
        }

        sort(cost1.rbegin(), cost1.rend());
        sort(cost2.rbegin(), cost2.rend());
        sort(cost3.rbegin(), cost3.rend());

        TurnData td = {};

        // m=0
        td.possible[0] = true;
        td.sum_d[0] = 0;
        td.max_d[0] = 0;

        // m=1
        long long max_m1 = 0;
        if (!cost1.empty()) max_m1 = max(max_m1, (long long)cost1[0]);
        if (!cost2.empty()) max_m1 = max(max_m1, (long long)cost2[0]);
        if (!cost3.empty()) max_m1 = max(max_m1, (long long)cost3[0]);
        td.possible[1] = (max_m1 > 0);
        td.sum_d[1] = max_m1;
        td.max_d[1] = max_m1;

        // m=2
        long long sum_m2 = 0;
        long long max_m2 = 0;
        td.possible[2] = false;

        // Check case1: two 1-cost
        if (cost1.size() >= 2) {
            long long sum_case = cost1[0] + cost1[1];
            long long max_case = max(cost1[0], cost1[1]);
            if (sum_case > sum_m2) {
                sum_m2 = sum_case;
                max_m2 = max_case;
                td.possible[2] = true;
            }
        }

        // Check case2: 1-cost and 2-cost
        if (!cost1.empty() && !cost2.empty()) {
            long long sum_case = cost1[0] + cost2[0];
            long long max_case = max(cost1[0], cost2[0]);
            if (sum_case > sum_m2) {
                sum_m2 = sum_case;
                max_m2 = max_case;
                td.possible[2] = true;
            } else if (sum_case == sum_m2) {
                max_m2 = max(max_m2, max_case);
            }
        }

        if (td.possible[2]) {
            td.sum_d[2] = sum_m2;
            td.max_d[2] = max_m2;
        }

        // m=3
        td.possible[3] = (cost1.size() >= 3);
        if (td.possible[3]) {
            td.sum_d[3] = cost1[0] + cost1[1] + cost1[2];
            td.max_d[3] = max({cost1[0], cost1[1], cost1[2]});
        }

        turns.push_back(td);
    }

    vector<long long> dp(10, LLONG_MIN);
    dp[0] = 0;

    for (const auto& td : turns) {
        vector<long long> new_dp(10, LLONG_MIN);
        for (int prev_mod = 0; prev_mod < 10; ++prev_mod) {
            if (dp[prev_mod] == LLONG_MIN) continue;
            for (int m = 0; m <= 3; ++m) {
                if (!td.possible[m]) continue;
                int new_mod = (prev_mod + m) % 10;
                int s = s_table[m][prev_mod];
                long long damage = td.sum_d[m] + s * td.max_d[m];
                if (new_dp[new_mod] < dp[prev_mod] + damage) {
                    new_dp[new_mod] = dp[prev_mod] + damage;
                }
            }
        }
        dp.swap(new_dp);
    }

    long long ans = *max_element(dp.begin(), dp.end());
    cout << ans << "\n";

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

struct DSU {
    vector<int> parent, rank, max_diam;

    DSU(int n) : parent(n+1), rank(n+1, 1), max_diam(n+1, 0) {
        iota(parent.begin(), parent.end(), 0);
    }

    int find(int x) {
        if (parent[x] != x) {
            parent[x] = find(parent[x]);
        }
        return parent[x];
    }

    void unite(int x, int y) {
        x = find(x);
        y = find(y);
        if (x == y) return;

        int d1 = max_diam[x];
        int d2 = max_diam[y];
        int r1 = (d1 + 1) / 2;
        int r2 = (d2 + 1) / 2;
        int new_diam = max({d1, d2, r1 + r2 + 1});

        if (rank[x] < rank[y]) {
            swap(x, y);
        }
        parent[y] = x;
        max_diam[x] = new_diam;
        if (rank[x] == rank[y]) {
            rank[x]++;
        }
    }
};

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    int n, m, q;
    cin >> n >> m >> q;

    vector<vector<int>> adj(n+1);
    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        int a, b;
        cin >> a >> b;
        adj[a].push_back(b);
        adj[b].push_back(a);
    }

    DSU dsu(n);
    vector<bool> visited(n+1, false);

    for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) {
        if (!visited[i]) {
            queue<int> q;
            q.push(i);
            visited[i] = true;
            vector<int> component = {i};

            while (!q.empty()) {
                int u = q.front();
                q.pop();
                for (int v : adj[u]) {
                    if (!visited[v]) {
                        visited[v] = true;
                        q.push(v);
                        component.push_back(v);
                    }
                }
            }

            int u = component[0];
            int max_dist = 0, farthest = u;
            queue<int> bfs_q;
            vector<int> dist(n+1, -1);
            bfs_q.push(u);
            dist[u] = 0;
            while (!bfs_q.empty()) {
                int x = bfs_q.front();
                bfs_q.pop();
                for (int y : adj[x]) {
                    if (dist[y] == -1) {
                        dist[y] = dist[x] + 1;
                        if (dist[y] > max_dist) {
                            max_dist = dist[y];
                            farthest = y;
                        }
                        bfs_q.push(y);
                    }
                }
            }

            u = farthest;
            max_dist = 0;
            bfs_q = queue<int>();
            fill(dist.begin(), dist.end(), -1);
            bfs_q.push(u);
            dist[u] = 0;
            while (!bfs_q.empty()) {
                int x = bfs_q.front();
                bfs_q.pop();
                for (int y : adj[x]) {
                    if (dist[y] == -1) {
                        dist[y] = dist[x] + 1;
                        if (dist[y] > max_dist) {
                            max_dist = dist[y];
                        }
                        bfs_q.push(y);
                    }
                }
            }

            int diameter = max_dist;
            int root = component[0];
            for (int node : component) {
                dsu.parent[node] = root;
            }
            dsu.rank[root] = component.size();
            dsu.max_diam[root] = diameter;
        }
    }

    while (q--) {
        int type, x, y;
        cin >> type;
        if (type == 1) {
            cin >> x;
            int root = dsu.find(x);
            cout << dsu.max_diam[root] << '\n';
        } else {
            cin >> x >> y;
            dsu.unite(x, y);
        }
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

vector<int> prefix_incr, prefix_decr;

bool is_peak(int a, int b, int c) {
    return a < b && b > c;
}

bool is_valley(int a, int b, int c) {
    return a > b && b < c;
}

bool is_peak_or_valley(int a, int b, int c) {
    return is_peak(a, b, c) || is_valley(a, b, c);
}

vector<int> find_quadruple(int L, int R, const vector<int>& a) {
    for (int i = L; i <= R-3; ++i) {
        bool first_peak = is_peak(a[i], a[i+1], a[i+2]);
        bool first_valley = is_valley(a[i], a[i+1], a[i+2]);
        if (!first_peak && !first_valley) continue;
        bool second_peak = is_peak(a[i+1], a[i+2], a[i+3]);
        bool second_valley = is_valley(a[i+1], a[i+2], a[i+3]);
        if ((first_peak && second_valley) || (first_valley && second_peak)) {
            return {i, i+1, i+2, i+3};
        }
    }
    return {};
}

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);
    int n, q;
    cin >> n >> q;
    vector<int> a(n);
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        cin >> a[i];
    }
    prefix_incr.resize(n);
    prefix_decr.resize(n);
    for (int i = 0; i < n-1; ++i) {
        prefix_incr[i+1] = prefix_incr[i] + (a[i] <= a[i+1] ? 1 : 0);
        prefix_decr[i+1] = prefix_decr[i] + (a[i] >= a[i+1] ? 1 : 0);
    }
    while (q--) {
        int L, R;
        cin >> L >> R;
        L--; R--;
        int len = R - L + 1;
        if (len < 3) {
            cout << "0\n";
            continue;
        }
        int sum_incr = prefix_incr[R] - (L == 0 ? 0 : prefix_incr[L-1]);
        bool is_incr = (sum_incr == (R - L + 1));
        int sum_decr = prefix_decr[R] - (L == 0 ? 0 : prefix_decr[L-1]);
        bool is_decr = (sum_decr == (R - L + 1));
        if (is_incr || is_decr) {
            cout << "0\n";
            continue;
        }
        if (len == 3) {
            cout << "3\n";
            cout << L+1 << ' ' << L+2 << ' ' << R+1 << '\n';
            continue;
        }
        vector<int> quad = find_quadruple(L, R, a);
        if (!quad.empty()) {
            cout << "4\n";
            for (int x : quad) cout << x+1 << ' ';
            cout << '\n';
            continue;
        }
        bool found = false;
        for (int i = L; i <= R-2; ++i) {
            if (is_peak_or_valley(a[i], a[i+1], a[i+2])) {
                cout << "3\n";
                cout << i+1 << ' ' << i+2 << ' ' << i+3 << '\n';
                found = true;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (!found) cout << "0\n";
    }
    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <iostream>
#include <vector>
#include <queue>
#include <cmath>
#include <iomanip>
using namespace std;

const double EPS = 1e-8;

struct EdgeInfo {
    int u, v;
    double a, b, c, d;
};

struct Edge {
    int to, rev;
    double cap;
    Edge(int to_, int rev_, double cap_) : to(to_), rev(rev_), cap(cap_) {}
};

class Dinic {
public:
    vector<vector<Edge>> g;
    vector<int> level, iter;
    Dinic(int n) {
        g.resize(n);
        level.resize(n);
        iter.resize(n);
    }
    void add_edge(int from, int to, double cap) {
        g[from].emplace_back(to, g[to].size(), cap);
        g[to].emplace_back(from, g[from].size()-1, 0.0);
    }
    void bfs(int s) {
        fill(level.begin(), level.end(), -1);
        queue<int> q;
        level[s] = 0;
        q.push(s);
        while (!q.empty()) {
            int v = q.front();
            q.pop();
            for (Edge& e : g[v]) {
                if (e.cap > EPS && level[e.to] == -1) {
                    level[e.to] = level[v] + 1;
                    q.push(e.to);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    double dfs(int v, int t, double f) {
        if (v == t) return f;
        for (int& i = iter[v]; i < g[v].size(); ++i) {
            Edge& e = g[v][i];
            if (e.cap > EPS && level[v] < level[e.to]) {
                double d = dfs(e.to, t, min(f, e.cap));
                if (d > EPS) {
                    e.cap -= d;
                    g[e.to][e.rev].cap += d;
                    return d;
                }
            }
        }
        return 0.0;
    }
    double max_flow(int s, int t) {
        double flow = 0.0;
        while (true) {
            bfs(s);
            if (level[t] == -1) return flow;
            fill(iter.begin(), iter.end(), 0);
            double f;
            while ((f = dfs(s, t, 1e18)) > EPS) {
                flow += f;
            }
        }
    }
};

vector<EdgeInfo> edges;
int n, m;

bool is_feasible(double t) {
    vector<double> l(m), r(m);
    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        EdgeInfo& e = edges[i];
        l[i] = e.a * t + e.b;
        r[i] = e.c * t + e.d;
    }

    vector<double> D(n+1, 0.0);
    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        EdgeInfo& e = edges[i];
        D[e.u] -= l[i];
        D[e.v] += l[i];
    }

    double sum_positive = 0.0;
    int S = 0, T = n+1;
    Dinic dinic(n+2);

    for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) {
        if (D[i] > EPS) {
            dinic.add_edge(S, i, D[i]);
            sum_positive += D[i];
        } else if (D[i] < -EPS) {
            dinic.add_edge(i, T, -D[i]);
        }
    }

    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        EdgeInfo& e = edges[i];
        double capacity = r[i] - l[i];
        if (capacity > EPS) {
            dinic.add_edge(e.u, e.v, capacity);
        }
    }

    double flow = dinic.max_flow(S, T);
    return abs(flow - sum_positive) < EPS;
}

double find_low() {
    double left = 0.0, right = 1.0;
    for (int iter = 0; iter < 100; ++iter) {
        double mid = (left + right) / 2;
        if (is_feasible(mid)) right = mid;
        else left = mid;
    }
    return right;
}

double find_high() {
    double left = 0.0, right = 1.0;
    for (int iter = 0; iter < 100; ++iter) {
        double mid = (left + right) / 2;
        if (is_feasible(mid)) left = mid;
        else right = mid;
    }
    return left;
}

    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0);

    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        cin >> edges[i].u >> edges[i].v >> edges[i].a >> edges[i].b >> edges[i].c >> edges[i].d;
    }

    double low = find_low();
    double high = find_high();

    double prob = max(0.0, high - low);
    cout << prob << endl;

#include <iostream>
#include <vector>
#include <map>
#include <algorithm>
#include <utility>

using namespace std;

void solve() {
    int n, m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    vector<vector<int>> adj(n + 1);
    vector<int> degree(n + 1, 0);
    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        int a, b;
        cin >> a >> b;
        adj[a].push_back(b);
        adj[b].push_back(a);
        degree[a]++;
        degree[b]++;
    }

    // Case 1: Check for a node with at least three leaves
    for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u) {
        vector<int> leaves;
        for (int v : adj[u]) {
            if (degree[v] == 1) {
                leaves.push_back(v);
            }
        }
        if (leaves.size() >= 3) {
            int c = 3;
            if (c < n) {
                cout << "Yes\n" << c << "\n";
                cout << leaves[0] << " " << leaves[1] << " " << leaves[2] << "\n";
                return;
            }
        }
    }

    // Case 2: Check for pairs with three common neighbors of degree 2
    map<pair<int, int>, vector<int>> common;
    for (int w = 1; w <= n; ++w) {
        if (degree[w] == 2) {
            int u = adj[w][0];
            int v = adj[w][1];
            if (u > v) swap(u, v);
            common[make_pair(u, v)].push_back(w);
        }
    }
    for (const auto& entry : common) {
        const auto& nodes = entry.second;
        if (nodes.size() >= 3) {
            int c = 3;
            if (c < n) {
                cout << "Yes\n" << c << "\n";
                cout << nodes[0] << " " << nodes[1] << " " << nodes[2] << "\n";
                return;
            }
        }
    }

    // Case 3: Check for a node with degree divisible by 3 and all leaves
    for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u) {
        if (degree[u] % 3 != 0) continue;
        bool all_leaves = true;
        vector<int> steal_list;
        for (int v : adj[u]) {
            if (degree[v] != 1) {
                all_leaves = false;
                break;
            }
            steal_list.push_back(v);
        }
        if (all_leaves && !steal_list.empty() && steal_list.size() < n) {
            cout << "Yes\n" << steal_list.size() << "\n";
            for (size_t i = 0; i < steal_list.size(); ++i) {
                if (i > 0) cout << " ";
                cout << steal_list[i];
            }
            cout << "\n";
            return;
        }
    }

    cout << "No\n";
}

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);
    int t;
    cin >> t;
    while (t--) {
        solve();
    }
    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

vector<int> compute_k_core(int n, const vector<unordered_set<int>>& adj, int k) {
    vector<int> deg(n + 1);
    for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u)
        deg[u] = adj[u].size();
    vector<bool> removed(n + 1, false);
    queue<int> q;
    for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u) {
        if (deg[u] < k) {
            q.push(u);
            removed[u] = true;
        }
    }
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.front();
        q.pop();
        for (int v : adj[u]) {
            if (!removed[v] && --deg[v] < k) {
                if (!removed[v]) {
                    removed[v] = true;
                    q.push(v);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    vector<int> core;
    for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u)
        if (!removed[u])
            core.push_back(u);
    return core;
}

bool check_clique(const vector<unordered_set<int>>& adj, const vector<int>& nodes) {
    for (int i = 0; i < nodes.size(); ++i) {
        for (int j = i + 1; j < nodes.size(); ++j) {
            if (!adj[nodes[i]].count(nodes[j]))
                return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

bool find_clique(int k, int u, const vector<int>& neighbors, const vector<unordered_set<int>>& adj, vector<int>& clique) {
    int n = neighbors.size();
    if (n < k - 1) return false;
    vector<int> indices(k - 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < k - 1; ++i)
        indices[i] = i;
    while (true) {
        vector<int> subset;
        subset.push_back(u);
        for (int i : indices)
            subset.push_back(neighbors[i]);
        if (check_clique(adj, subset)) {
            clique = subset;
            return true;
        }
        int pos = k - 2;
        while (pos >= 0 && indices[pos] == n - (k - 1 - pos))
            --pos;
        if (pos < 0)
            break;
        indices[pos]++;
        for (int j = pos + 1; j < k - 1; ++j)
            indices[j] = indices[j - 1] + 1;
    }
    return false;
}

void solve() {
    int n, m, k;
    cin >> n >> m >> k;
    vector<unordered_set<int>> adj(n + 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        int u, v;
        cin >> u >> v;
        adj[u].insert(v);
        adj[v].insert(u);
    }
    vector<int> core = compute_k_core(n, adj, k);
    if (!core.empty()) {
        cout << "1 " << core.size() << '\n';
        for (int u : core)
            cout << u << ' ';
        cout << '\n';
        return;
    }
    if (k == 1) {
        cout << "1 1\n1\n";
        return;
    }
    if (k == 2) {
        for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u) {
            if (!adj[u].empty()) {
                int v = *adj[u].begin();
                cout << "2\n" << u << ' ' << v << '\n';
                return;
            }
        }
        cout << "-1\n";
        return;
    }
    vector<int> all_nodes(n);
    iota(all_nodes.begin(), all_nodes.end(), 1);
    for (int u : all_nodes) {
        if (adj[u].size() < k - 1)
            continue;
        vector<int> neighbors(adj[u].begin(), adj[u].end());
        vector<int> clique;
        if (find_clique(k, u, neighbors, adj, clique)) {
            cout << "2\n";
            for (int x : clique)
                cout << x << ' ';
            cout << '\n';
            return;
        }
    }
    cout << "-1\n";
}

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0);
    int t;
    cin >> t;
    while (t--)
        solve();
    return 0;

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;
using i64 = long long;

vector<vector<pair<int, int>>> adj, adj_rev;
vector<int> order, component;
vector<bool> visited;

void dfs1(int u) {
    visited[u] = true;
    for (auto [v, l] : adj[u]) {
        if (!visited[v]) {
            dfs1(v);
        }
    }
    order.push_back(u);
}

void dfs2(int u, int label) {
    component[u] = label;
    for (auto [v, l] : adj_rev[u]) {
        if (component[v] == -1) {
            dfs2(v, label);
        }
    }
}

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);
    int n, m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    adj.resize(n+1);
    adj_rev.resize(n+1);
    for (int i=0; i<m; ++i) {
        int a, b, l;
        cin >> a >> b >> l;
        adj[a].emplace_back(b, l);
        adj_rev[b].emplace_back(a, l);
    }

    visited.assign(n+1, false);
    for (int u=1; u<=n; ++u) {
        if (!visited[u]) {
            dfs1(u);
        }
    }
    reverse(order.begin(), order.end());
    component.assign(n+1, -1);
    int label = 0;
    for (int u : order) {
        if (component[u] == -1) {
            dfs2(u, label);
            label++;
        }
    }

    vector<vector<int>> scc_nodes(label);
    for (int u=1; u<=n; ++u) {
        scc_nodes[component[u]].push_back(u);
    }

    vector<vector<tuple<int, int, int>>> comp_edges(label);
    for (int a=1; a<=n; ++a) {
        for (auto [b, l] : adj[a]) {
            if (component[a] == component[b]) {
                comp_edges[component[a]].emplace_back(a, b, l);
            }
        }
    }

    vector<i64> g(n+1, 0);
    for (int c=0; c<label; ++c) {
        const auto& nodes = scc_nodes[c];
        if (nodes.size() == 1) {
            g[nodes[0]] = 0;
            continue;
        }

        vector<vector<pair<int, int>>> adj_c(n+1);
        for (auto [a, b, l] : comp_edges[c]) {
            adj_c[a].emplace_back(b, l);
        }

        int root = nodes[0];
        vector<i64> d(n+1, 0);
        vector<bool> visited_c(n+1, false);
        queue<int> q;
        q.push(root);
        visited_c[root] = true;
        d[root] = 0;

        while (!q.empty()) {
            int u = q.front();
            q.pop();
            for (auto [v, l] : adj_c[u]) {
                if (!visited_c[v]) {
                    visited_c[v] = true;
                    d[v] = d[u] + l;
                    q.push(v);
                }
            }
        }

        i64 current_gcd = 0;
        for (auto [a, b, l] : comp_edges[c]) {
            i64 discrepancy = d[a] + l - d[b];
            current_gcd = gcd(current_gcd, abs(discrepancy));
        }

        for (int u : nodes) {
            g[u] = current_gcd;
        }
    }

    int q_cnt;
    cin >> q_cnt;
    while (q_cnt--) {
        int v, s, t;
        cin >> v >> s >> t;
        i64 g_v = g[v];
        if (g_v == 0) {
            cout << (s == 0 ? "YES\n" : "NO\n");
        } else {
            i64 d_gcd = gcd(g_v, t);
            i64 required = (t - s) % t;
            cout << (required % d_gcd == 0 ? "YES\n" : "NO\n");
        }
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <string>
#include <algorithm>
#include <iostream>
using namespace std;

const int MOD = 1e9 + 7;
const int MOD_PHI = MOD - 1;

int mod_str(const string &s, int m) {
    int res = 0;
    for (char c : s) {
        res = (res * 10LL + (c - '0')) % m;
    }
    return res;
}

int pow_mod(int a, int b, int m) {
    int res = 1;
    a %= m;
    while (b) {
        if (b & 1) res = (long long)res * a % m;
        a = (long long)a * a % m;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}

int mod_inverse(int a, int m) {
    return pow_mod(a, m - 2, m);
}

string increment(string s) {
    reverse(s.begin(), s.end());
    int carry = 1;
    for (int i = 0; i < s.size() && carry; ++i) {
        int digit = (s[i] - '0') + carry;
        if (digit >= 10) {
            carry = 1;
            digit -= 10;
        } else {
            carry = 0;
        }
        s[i] = '0' + digit;
    }
    if (carry) {
        s.push_back('1');
    }
    reverse(s.begin(), s.end());
    return s;
}

bool compare(const string &a, const string &b) {
    if (a.size() != b.size()) return a.size() < b.size();
    return a <= b;
}

struct Result {
    int value;
    int length_mod;
};

struct NumberResult {
    string s;
    int value;
    int length_mod;
};

NumberResult parse_number(int &pos, const string &s) {
    int start = pos;
    if (s[pos] == '0') {
        pos++;
        return {"0", 0, 1 % MOD_PHI};
    }
    while (pos < s.size() && isdigit(s[pos])) pos++;
    string num_str = s.substr(start, pos - start);
    int value = mod_str(num_str, MOD);
    int length_mod = (int)num_str.size() % MOD_PHI;
    return {num_str, value, length_mod};
}

Result parse_expression(int &pos, const string &s);

Result parse_term(int &pos, const string &s) {
    NumberResult nr = parse_number(pos, s);
    if (pos < s.size() && s[pos] == '-') {
        pos++;
        NumberResult r_nr = parse_number(pos, s);
        string l_str = nr.s;
        string r_str = r_nr.s;
        int value = 0;
        int length_mod = 0;
        string current = l_str;
        while (compare(current, r_str)) {
            for (char c : current) {
                value = (value * 10LL + (c - '0')) % MOD;
                length_mod = (length_mod + 1) % MOD_PHI;
            }
            if (current == r_str) break;
            current = increment(current);
        }
        return {value, length_mod};
    } else if (pos < s.size() && s[pos] == '(') {
        pos++;
        Result expr = parse_expression(pos, s);
        pos++;
        string K_str = nr.s;
        int l = expr.length_mod;
        int v = expr.value;
        int a = pow_mod(10, l, MOD);
        int sum;
        if (a == 1) {
            sum = mod_str(K_str, MOD) % MOD;
        } else {
            int K_mod = mod_str(K_str, MOD_PHI);
            int a_pow = pow_mod(a, K_mod, MOD);
            int numerator = (a_pow - 1 + MOD) % MOD;
            int denominator = (a - 1 + MOD) % MOD;
            int inv_denominator = mod_inverse(denominator, MOD);
            sum = (long long)numerator * inv_denominator % MOD;
        }
        int total_value = (long long)v * sum % MOD;
        int K_mod_length = mod_str(K_str, MOD_PHI);
        int total_length = (long long)l * K_mod_length % MOD_PHI;
        return {total_value, total_length};
    } else {
        return {nr.value, nr.length_mod};
    }
}

Result parse_expression(int &pos, const string &s) {
    Result res = parse_term(pos, s);
    while (pos < s.size() && s[pos] == '+') {
        pos++;
        Result term = parse_term(pos, s);
        int pow_10 = pow_mod(10, term.length_mod, MOD);
        res.value = ((long long)res.value * pow_10 + term.value) % MOD;
        res.length_mod = (res.length_mod + term.length_mod) % MOD_PHI;
    }
    return res;
}

int main() {
    string s;
    cin >> s;
    int pos = 0;
    Result result = parse_expression(pos, s);
    cout << result.value << endl;
    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <vector>
#include <queue>
#include <iostream>
#include <tuple>
#include <algorithm>
using namespace std;

pair<int, vector<int>> solve(const vector<vector<pair<int, char>>>& adj, int n, char target) {
    vector<bool> visited(n+1, false);
    vector<int> x(n+1, -1);
    vector<int> flips;
    bool possible = true;

    for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u) {
        if (!visited[u]) {
            queue<int> q;
            q.push(u);
            visited[u] = true;
            x[u] = 0;
            vector<int> component;
            component.push_back(u);
            int cnt_ones = 0;

            while (!q.empty()) {
                int current = q.front();
                q.pop();
                cnt_ones += x[current];

                for (auto [v, c] : adj[current]) {
                    bool t_i = (c != target);
                    int desired = x[current] ^ t_i;

                    if (!visited[v]) {
                        visited[v] = true;
                        x[v] = desired;
                        q.push(v);
                        component.push_back(v);
                    } else {
                        if (x[v] != desired) {
                            possible = false;
                            while (!q.empty()) q.pop();
                            goto end_component;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            end_component:
            if (!possible) break;

            int m = min(cnt_ones, (int)component.size() - cnt_ones);
            if (cnt_ones <= (int)component.size() - cnt_ones) {
                for (int node : component) {
                    if (x[node] == 1) {
                        flips.push_back(node);
                    }
                }
            } else {
                for (int node : component) {
                    if (x[node] == 0) {
                        flips.push_back(node);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    if (possible) {
        return {flips.size(), flips};
    } else {
        return {-1, {}};
    }
}

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    int n, m;
    cin >> n >> m;

    vector<vector<pair<int, char>>> adj(n+1);
    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        int u, v;
        char c;
        cin >> u >> v >> c;
        adj[u].emplace_back(v, c);
        adj[v].emplace_back(u, c);
    }

    auto res_r = solve(adj, n, 'R');
    auto res_b = solve(adj, n, 'B');

    int best = -1;
    vector<int> answer;

    if (res_r.first != -1) {
        best = res_r.first;
        answer = res_r.second;
    }
    if (res_b.first != -1) {
        if (best == -1 || res_b.first < best) {
            best = res_b.first;
            answer = res_b.second;
        }
    }

    if (best == -1) {
        cout << -1 << '\n';
    } else {
        cout << best << '\n';
        for (size_t i = 0; i < answer.size(); ++i) {
            if (i > 0) cout << ' ';
            cout << answer[i];
        }
        cout << '\n';
    }

    return 0;

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

void findBridges(int u, int parent, int &time, vector<int>& disc, vector<int>& low, vector<bool>& visited, const vector<vector<int>>& adj, set<pair<int, int>>& bridges) {
    visited[u] = true;
    disc[u] = low[u] = time++;
    for (int v : adj[u]) {
        if (v == parent) continue;
        if (!visited[v]) {
            findBridges(v, u, time, disc, low, visited, adj, bridges);
            low[u] = min(low[u], low[v]);
            if (low[v] > disc[u]) {
                bridges.insert({min(u, v), max(u, v)});
            }
        } else {
            low[u] = min(low[u], disc[v]);
        }
    }
}

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    int n, m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    vector<vector<int>> adj(n + 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        int a, b;
        cin >> a >> b;
        adj[a].push_back(b);
        adj[b].push_back(a);
    }

    set<pair<int, int>> bridges;
    vector<int> disc(n + 1), low(n + 1);
    vector<bool> visited(n + 1, false);
    int time = 0;

    for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u) {
        if (!visited[u]) {
            findBridges(u, -1, time, disc, low, visited, adj, bridges);
        }
    }

    vector<int> comp(n + 1, -1);
    int current_comp = 0;
    for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u) {
        if (comp[u] == -1) {
            queue<int> q;
            q.push(u);
            comp[u] = current_comp;
            while (!q.empty()) {
                int v = q.front();
                q.pop();
                for (int w : adj[v]) {
                    if (comp[w] == -1 && !bridges.count({min(v, w), max(v, w)})) {
                        comp[w] = current_comp;
                        q.push(w);
                    }
                }
            }
            current_comp++;
        }
    }

    if (current_comp == 1) {
        cout << 0 << endl;
        return 0;
    }

    vector<unordered_set<int>> bridge_tree(current_comp);
    for (auto& bridge : bridges) {
        int u = bridge.first;
        int v = bridge.second;
        int cu = comp[u];
        int cv = comp[v];
        bridge_tree[cu].insert(cv);
        bridge_tree[cv].insert(cu);
    }

    vector<int> leaves;
    for (int i = 0; i < current_comp; ++i) {
        if (bridge_tree[i].size() == 1) {
            leaves.push_back(i);
        }
    }

    if (leaves.empty()) {
        cout << 0 << endl;
        return 0;
    }

    vector<vector<int>> comp_nodes(current_comp);
    for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u) {
        comp_nodes[comp[u]].push_back(u);
    }

    vector<int> reps;
    for (int leaf_comp : leaves) {
        reps.push_back(comp_nodes[leaf_comp][0]);
    }

    int t = (reps.size() + 1) / 2;
    cout << t << endl;

    for (int i = 0; i < t; ++i) {
        int j = (i + t) % reps.size();
        cout << reps[i] << " " << reps[j] << endl;
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

const int MOD = 1e9 + 7;
const int MAXN = 1e5 + 10;

long long fact[MAXN];

void precompute_factorials() {
    fact[0] = 1;
    for (int i = 1; i < MAXN; ++i) {
        fact[i] = fact[i - 1] * i % MOD;
    }
}

struct CheckResult {
    bool valid;
    int components;
    int total_nodes;
};

CheckResult check_subset(const vector<pair<int, int>>& edges) {
    unordered_map<int, vector<int>> adj;
    unordered_set<int> nodes;

    for (const auto& e : edges) {
        int u = e.first, v = e.second;
        adj[u].push_back(v);
        adj[v].push_back(u);
        nodes.insert(u);
        nodes.insert(v);
    }

    for (int node : nodes) {
        if (adj[node].size() > 2) {
            return {false, 0, 0};
        }
    }

    unordered_set<int> visited;
    int components = 0;
    bool valid = true;

    for (int node : nodes) {
        if (visited.count(node)) continue;
        components++;
        queue<int> q;
        q.push(node);
        visited.insert(node);
        int m = 0;
        int sum_degree = 0;

        while (!q.empty()) {
            int u = q.front();
            q.pop();
            m++;
            sum_degree += adj[u].size();

            for (int v : adj[u]) {
                if (!visited.count(v)) {
                    visited.insert(v);
                    q.push(v);
                }
            }
        }

        if (sum_degree % 2 != 0 || sum_degree / 2 != m - 1) {
            valid = false;
        }
    }

    if (!valid) {
        return {false, 0, 0};
    }

    return {true, components, (int)nodes.size()};
}

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0);

    precompute_factorials();

    int T;
    cin >> T;

    while (T--) {
        int N, M;
        cin >> N >> M;
        vector<pair<int, int>> edges(M);
        for (int i = 0; i < M; ++i) {
            int u, v;
            cin >> u >> v;
            if (u > v) swap(u, v);
            edges[i] = {u, v};
        }

        long long ans = 0;

        for (int mask = 0; mask < (1 << M); ++mask) {
            vector<pair<int, int>> subset;
            for (int i = 0; i < M; ++i) {
                if (mask & (1 << i)) {
                    subset.push_back(edges[i]);
                }
            }

            auto [valid, components, total_nodes] = check_subset(subset);
            if (!valid) continue;

            int cnt = __builtin_popcount(mask);
            int m_blocks = N - total_nodes + components;

            if (m_blocks < 0) continue;
            if (m_blocks >= MAXN) {
                // Handle cases where m_blocks exceeds precomputed factorials (unlikely as N is 1e5)
                // For this problem, N is up to 1e5 and M up to 7, so m_blocks is up to 1e5 + 7, which is covered by MAXN
                continue;
            }

            long long pow2 = 1LL << components;
            pow2 %= MOD;

            long long term = fact[m_blocks] * pow2 % MOD;
            if (cnt % 2 == 1) {
                term = MOD - term;
            }

            ans = (ans + term) % MOD;
        }

        cout << (ans % MOD + MOD) % MOD << '\n';
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;
using ll = long long;

struct Edge {
    int a, b;
    ll l;
};

ll gcd(ll a, ll b) {
    a = abs(a);
    b = abs(b);
    while (b) {
        a %= b;
        swap(a, b);
    }
    return a;
}

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    int n, m;
    cin >> n >> m;

    vector<Edge> edges(m);
    vector<vector<int>> adj(n+1), adj_t(n+1);

    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        int a, b;
        ll l;
        cin >> a >> b >> l;
        edges[i] = {a, b, l};
        adj[a].push_back(b);
        adj_t[b].push_back(a);
    }

    // Kosaraju's algorithm to find SCCs
    vector<int> order;
    vector<bool> visited(n+1, false);

    for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) {
        if (!visited[i]) {
            stack<int> st;
            st.push(i);
            visited[i] = true;
            while (!st.empty()) {
                int u = st.top();
                bool found = false;
                for (int v : adj[u]) {
                    if (!visited[v]) {
                        visited[v] = true;
                        st.push(v);
                        found = true;
                        break;
                    }
                }
                if (!found) {
                    st.pop();
                    order.push_back(u);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    reverse(order.begin(), order.end());
    fill(visited.begin(), visited.end(), false);
    vector<int> component(n+1, -1);
    int current_component = 0;
    vector<vector<int>> components;

    for (int u : order) {
        if (!visited[u]) {
            stack<int> st;
            st.push(u);
            visited[u] = true;
            component[u] = current_component;
            vector<int> current_nodes;
            current_nodes.push_back(u);
            while (!st.empty()) {
                int v = st.top();
                st.pop();
                for (int w : adj_t[v]) {
                    if (!visited[w]) {
                        visited[w] = true;
                        component[w] = current_component;
                        st.push(w);
                        current_nodes.push_back(w);
                    }
                }
            }
            components.push_back(current_nodes);
            current_component++;
        }
    }

    // Build component edges
    vector<vector<Edge>> component_edges(current_component);
    for (auto& e : edges) {
        int a = e.a, b = e.b;
        if (component[a] == component[b]) {
            component_edges[component[a]].push_back(e);
        }
    }

    // Compute cycle_gcd for each component
    vector<ll> cycle_gcd(current_component, 0);

    for (int c = 0; c < current_component; ++c) {
        const auto& comp_edges = component_edges[c];
        if (comp_edges.empty()) {
            cycle_gcd[c] = 0;
            continue;
        }

        unordered_map<int, vector<pair<int, ll>>> adj_comp;
        for (const auto& e : comp_edges) {
            adj_comp[e.a].emplace_back(e.b, e.l);
        }

        const auto& nodes = components[c];
        if (nodes.empty()) {
            cycle_gcd[c] = 0;
            continue;
        }

        int root = nodes[0];
        unordered_map<int, ll> d;
        d[root] = 0;
        queue<int> q;
        q.push(root);
        ll g = 0;

        while (!q.empty()) {
            int u = q.front();
            q.pop();

            if (adj_comp.find(u) == adj_comp.end()) continue;

            for (const auto& edge : adj_comp[u]) {
                int v = edge.first;
                ll l = edge.second;

                if (d.find(v) == d.end()) {
                    d[v] = d[u] + l;
                    q.push(v);
                } else {
                    ll candidate = d[u] + l - d[v];
                    g = gcd(g, candidate);
                }
            }
        }

        cycle_gcd[c] = g;
    }

    // Process queries
    int q;
    cin >> q;
    while (q--) {
        int v, s, t;
        cin >> v >> s >> t;

        if (s % t == 0) {
            cout << "YES\n";
            continue;
        }

        int c = component[v];
        ll g = cycle_gcd[c];

        if (g == 0) {
            cout << "NO\n";
            continue;
        }

        ll d = gcd(g, t);
        if (s % d == 0) {
            cout << "YES\n";
        } else {
            cout << "NO\n";
        }
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

const int MOD = 998244353;

struct FenwickTree {
    vector<int> tree;
    int n;

    FenwickTree(int size) : n(size), tree(size + 2, 0) {}

    void update(int idx, int delta) {
        for (; idx <= n; idx += idx & -idx)
            tree[idx] += delta;
    }

    int query(int idx) {
        int res = 0;
        for (; idx > 0; idx -= idx & -idx)
            res += tree[idx];
        return res;
    }
};

long long mod_pow(long long a, long long b) {
    long long res = 1;
    while (b > 0) {
        if (b % 2 == 1)
            res = res * a % MOD;
        a = a * a % MOD;
        b /= 2;
    }
    return res;
}

int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    vector<int> p(n);
    vector<int> known_values;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        cin >> p[i];
        if (p[i] != -1) {
            known_values.push_back(p[i]);
        }
    }

    set<int> known_set(known_values.begin(), known_values.end());
    vector<int> S;
    for (int x = 1; x <= n; x++) {
        if (known_set.find(x) == known_set.end()) {
            S.push_back(x);
        }
    }
    sort(S.begin(), S.end());
    int m = S.size();

    int sum_known = 0;
    int k = known_values.size();
    if (k > 0) {
        vector<int> sorted = known_values;
        sort(sorted.begin(), sorted.end());
        map<int, int> rank;
        for (int i = 0; i < sorted.size(); i++) {
            rank[sorted[i]] = i + 1;
        }
        FenwickTree ft(sorted.size());
        for (int x : known_values) {
            int r = rank[x];
            int current = ft.query(ft.n);
            sum_known = (sum_known + (current - ft.query(r))) % MOD;
            ft.update(r, 1);
        }
    }

    vector<int> prefix(n + 1, 0);
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        prefix[i+1] = prefix[i] + (p[i] == -1 ? 1 : 0);
    }

    long long sum_cross = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        if (p[i] == -1) continue;
        int x = p[i];
        int left = prefix[i];
        int right = prefix[n] - prefix[i+1];

        auto it_less = lower_bound(S.begin(), S.end(), x);
        int c_less = it_less - S.begin();

        auto it_greater = upper_bound(S.begin(), S.end(), x);
        int c_greater = S.end() - it_greater;

        sum_cross = (sum_cross + 1LL * left * c_greater) % MOD;
        sum_cross = (sum_cross + 1LL * right * c_less) % MOD;
    }
    if (m != 0) {
        long long inv_m = mod_pow(m, MOD - 2);
        sum_cross = sum_cross * inv_m % MOD;
    }

    long long sum_unknown = 0;
    if (m >= 2) {
        long long inv_4 = mod_pow(4, MOD - 2);
        sum_unknown = (1LL * m * (m - 1)) % MOD;
        sum_unknown = sum_unknown * inv_4 % MOD;
    }

    long long total = (sum_known + sum_cross + sum_unknown) % MOD;
    cout << total << endl;

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <vector>

using namespace std;

int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;

    vector<bool> used(n + 1, false);
    vector<int> primes;

    if (n >= 2) {
        primes.push_back(2);
        vector<bool> is_prime(n + 1, true);
        is_prime[0] = is_prime[1] = false;
        for (int i = 3; i <= n; i += 2) {
            if (is_prime[i]) {
                primes.push_back(i);
                for (int j = i * i; j <= n; j += 2 * i) {
                    is_prime[j] = false;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    vector<int> evens;
    for (int i = 2; i <= n; i += 2) {
        evens.push_back(i);
    }
    int m_even = evens.size();
    vector<pair<int, int>> pairs;

    if (m_even >= 1) {
        if (m_even % 2 == 0) {
            for (int i = 0; i < m_even; i += 2) {
                pairs.emplace_back(evens[i], evens[i + 1]);
            }
            for (int x : evens) {
                used[x] = true;
            }
        } else {
            for (int i = 0; i < m_even - 1; i += 2) {
                pairs.emplace_back(evens[i], evens[i + 1]);
            }
            for (int i = 0; i < m_even - 1; ++i) {
                used[evens[i]] = true;
            }
        }
    }

    int max_even = (m_even % 2 == 1) ? evens.back() : -1;

    for (size_t i = 1; i < primes.size(); ++i) {
        int p = primes[i];
        vector<int> multiples;
        for (int x = p; x <= n; x += p) {
            if (!used[x]) {
                multiples.push_back(x);
            }
        }

        if (multiples.empty()) continue;

        if (multiples.size() % 2 == 0) {
            for (size_t j = 0; j < multiples.size(); j += 2) {
                pairs.emplace_back(multiples[j], multiples[j + 1]);
                used[multiples[j]] = true;
                used[multiples[j + 1]] = true;
            }
        } else {
            bool has_max_even = (max_even != -1) && (max_even % p == 0) && !used[max_even];
            if (has_max_even) {
                multiples.push_back(max_even);
                used[max_even] = true;
                max_even = -1;
                for (size_t j = 0; j < multiples.size(); j += 2) {
                    pairs.emplace_back(multiples[j], multiples[j + 1]);
                    used[multiples[j]] = true;
                    used[multiples[j + 1]] = true;
                }
            } else {
                for (size_t j = 0; j < multiples.size() - 1; j += 2) {
                    pairs.emplace_back(multiples[j], multiples[j + 1]);
                    used[multiples[j]] = true;
                    used[multiples[j + 1]] = true;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    cout << pairs.size() << endl;
    for (auto& p : pairs) {
        cout << p.first << " " << p.second << endl;
    }

    return 0;

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

vector<int> compute_k_core(int n, const vector<vector<int>>& adj, int k) {
    vector<int> degree(n + 1);
    for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u) {
        degree[u] = adj[u].size();
    }
    vector<bool> deleted(n + 1, false);
    queue<int> q;
    for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u) {
        if (degree[u] < k) {
            q.push(u);
            deleted[u] = true;
        }
    }
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.front();
        q.pop();
        for (int v : adj[u]) {
            if (!deleted[v]) {
                --degree[v];
                if (degree[v] < k && !deleted[v]) {
                    deleted[v] = true;
                    q.push(v);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    vector<int> core;
    for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u) {
        if (!deleted[u]) {
            core.push_back(u);
        }
    }
    return core;
}

void solve() {
    int n, m, k;
    cin >> n >> m >> k;
    vector<vector<int>> adj(n + 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        int u, v;
        cin >> u >> v;
        adj[u].push_back(v);
        adj[v].push_back(u);
    }
    vector<int> core = compute_k_core(n, adj, k);
    if (!core.empty()) {
        cout << "1 " << core.size() << '\n';
        for (int u : core) {
            cout << u << ' ';
        }
        cout << '\n';
        return;
    }
    if (k == 1) {
        cout << "1 1\n1\n";
        return;
    }
    if (k == 2) {
        for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u) {
            if (!adj[u].empty()) {
                cout << "2 2\n" << u << ' ' << adj[u][0] << '\n';
                return;
            }
        }
        cout << "-1\n";
        return;
    }
    int max_degree = 0;
    for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u) {
        max_degree = max(max_degree, (int)adj[u].size());
    }
    if (max_degree < k - 1) {
        cout << "-1\n";
        return;
    }
    vector<vector<int>> sorted_adj(n + 1);
    for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u) {
        sorted_adj[u] = adj[u];
        sort(sorted_adj[u].begin(), sorted_adj[u].end());
    }
    for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u) {
        if ((int)adj[u].size() < k - 1) continue;
        for (int v : adj[u]) {
            auto& a = sorted_adj[u];
            auto& b = sorted_adj[v];
            int i = 0, j = 0;
            vector<int> common;
            while (i < a.size() && j < b.size() && common.size() < k - 2) {
                if (a[i] == v) { i++; continue; }
                if (b[j] == u) { j++; continue; }
                if (a[i] == b[j]) {
                    common.push_back(a[i]);
                    i++;
                    j++;
                } else if (a[i] < b[j]) {
                    i++;
                } else {
                    j++;
                }
            }
            if (common.size() >= k - 2) {
                vector<int> clique = {u, v};
                for (int l = 0; l < k - 2; ++l) {
                    clique.push_back(common[l]);
                }
                cout << "2\n";
                for (int x : clique) {
                    cout << x << ' ';
                }
                cout << '\n';
                return;
            }
        }
    }
    cout << "-1\n";
}

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);
    int t;
    cin >> t;
    while (t--) {
        solve();
    }
    return 0;
