#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;
using ll = long long;

vector<ll> sieve(ll n) {
    vector<bool> is_prime(n+1, true);
    is_prime[0] = is_prime[1] = false;
    for (ll i = 2; i*i <= n; ++i) {
        if (is_prime[i]) {
            for (ll j = i*i; j <= n; j += i)
                is_prime[j] = false;
        }
    }
    vector<ll> res;
    for (ll i = 2; i <= n; ++i)
        if (is_prime[i])
            res.push_back(i);
    return res;
}

ll count_pi(ll n) {
    if (n < 2) return 0;
    ll v = sqrt(n), s = (v + 1) / 2;
    vector<ll> small(s), rough(s), lare(s);
    for (ll i = 0; i < s; ++i) {
        small[i] = i;
        rough[i] = 2*i + 1;
        lare[i] = (n/(2*i+1) -1)/2;
    }
    vector<bool> skip(v+1, false);
    ll pc = 0;
    for (ll p = 3; p <= v; p += 2) {
        if (skip[p]) continue;
        ll q = p*p;
        pc++;
        if (q > v) break;
        for (ll i = q; i <= v; i += 2*p)
            skip[i] = true;
    }
    ll ptr = 0;
    for (ll i = 1; i <= v; i += 2)
        if (!skip[i])
            rough[ptr++] = i;
    rough.resize(ptr);
    lare.resize(ptr);
    for (ll j = 0; j < ptr; ++j) {
        ll p = rough[j];
        if (p*p > n) break;
        ll cnt = 0;
        for (ll i = p; i <= v; i += 2*p)
            if (!skip[i]) cnt++;
        ll ns = 0;
        for (ll k = 0; k < ptr; ++k) {
            ll i = rough[k];
            if (skip[i]) continue;
            ll d = i*p;
            lare[k] -= (d <= v ? lare[(d-1)/2] : small.back()) - cnt;
            rough[ns++] = i;
        }
        ptr = ns;
        for (ll j = (v/p)|1; j >= p; j -= 2) {
            ll c = small[j/2];
            for (ll i = j*p; i <= v; i += 2*p) {
                if (small[i/2] < c) break;
                small[i/2] -= c;
            }
        }
    }
    ll res = lare[0] + ptr + 1;
    for (ll k = 1; k < ptr; ++k)
        res -= lare[k];
    for (ll l = 1; l < ptr; ++l) {
        ll q = rough[l];
        ll m = n / q;
        if (m <= v) break;
        ll cnt = 0;
        for (ll i = 0; i < ptr; ++i) {
            ll p = rough[i];
            if (p > m / q) break;
            cnt += small[(m/p)/2];
        }
        res -= cnt - l;
    }
    return res;
}

int main() {
    ll n;
    cin >> n;

    ll cube_count = 0;
    if (n >= 8) {
        ll cube_root = pow(n, 1.0/3);
        while ((cube_root + 1) * (cube_root + 1) * (cube_root + 1) <= n) cube_root++;
        cube_count = sieve(cube_root).size();
    }

    ll product_count = 0;
    if (n >= 6) {
        ll sqrt_n = sqrt(n);
        auto primes = sieve(sqrt_n);
        vector<ll> precomp = sieve(1e6);
        for (ll p : primes) {
            ll x = n / p;
            if (x <= p) continue;
            ll cnt;
            if (x <= 1e6) {
                auto it_low = upper_bound(precomp.begin(), precomp.end(), p);
                auto it_high = upper_bound(precomp.begin(), precomp.end(), x);
                cnt = it_high - it_low;
            } else {
                cnt = count_pi(x) - count_pi(p);
            }
            product_count += cnt;
        }
    }

    cout << cube_count + product_count << endl;
    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;
using ll = long long;

ll powmod(ll a, ll b, ll m) {
    ll res = 1;
    a %= m;
    while (b) {
        if (b & 1) res = res * a % m;
        a = a * a % m;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}

ll inv(ll a, ll m) {
    return powmod(a, m-2, m);
}

ll order(ll a, ll p) {
    ll ord = p - 1;
    ll temp = ord;
    for (ll i = 2; i * i <= temp; ++i) {
        if (temp % i) continue;
        while (ord % i == 0 && powmod(a, ord / i, p) == 1)
            ord /= i;
        while (temp % i == 0)
            temp /= i;
    }
    if (temp > 1) {
        if (powmod(a, ord / temp, p) == 1)
            ord /= temp;
    }
    return ord;
}

ll discrete_log(ll a, ll b, ll m) {
    a %= m;
    b %= m;
    if (a == 0) return b == 0 ? 1 : -1;
    ll k = 1, add = 0, g;
    while ((g = gcd(a, m)) > 1) {
        if (b == k) return add;
        if (b % g) return -1;
        b /= g;
        m /= g;
        add++;
        k = k * (a / g) % m;
    }
    ll n = sqrt(m) + 1;
    unordered_map<ll, ll> vals;
    for (ll q = 0, cur = b; q <= n; ++q) {
        vals[cur] = q;
        cur = cur * a % m;
    }
    ll an = powmod(a, n, m);
    for (ll p = 1, cur = k; p <= n; ++p) {
        cur = cur * an % m;
        if (vals.count(cur))
            return n * p - vals[cur] + add;
    }
    return -1;
}

bool solve(ll k, ll l, ll r, ll p) {
    if (p == 2) {
        if (k % 2 == 0) return 1 % 2 == 1;
        else {
            return (1 % 2 == 1);
        }
    }
    if (k % p == 0) {
        return 1 % p == 1;
    }
    ll d = (p - 1) * inv(k, p) % p;
    ll a = 2 % p;
    ll ord = order(a, p);
    ll m = discrete_log(a, d, p);
    if (m == -1) return false;
    ll min_m = m;
    while (min_m <= r) {
        if (min_m >= l) return true;
        min_m += ord;
    }
    return false;
}

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0);
    cout.tie(0);
    int t;
    cin >> t;
    while (t--) {
        ll k, l, r, p;
        cin >> k >> l >> r >> p;
        if (k % p == 0) {
            cout << 1 % p << '\n';
            continue;
        }
        if (p == 2) {
            cout << (1 % 2) << '\n';
            continue;
        }
        ll d = (p - 1) * inv(k % p, p) % p;
        ll a = 2 % p;
        ll ord = order(a, p);
        ll m = discrete_log(a, d, p);
        bool found = false;
        if (m != -1) {
            ll base = m;
            if (base > r) {
                found = false;
            } else {
                ll steps = (r - base) / ord;
                ll minimal = base + steps * ord;
                if (minimal >= l && minimal <= r) {
                    found = true;
                } else {
                    if (base >= l && base <= r)
                        found = true;
                    else {
                        ll candidate = base + ((l - base + ord - 1) / ord) * ord;
                        if (candidate <= r)
                            found = true;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        if (found) {
            cout << 0 << '\n';
        } else {
            cout << 1 << '\n';
        }
    }
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

struct Rectangle {
    int x1, y1, x2, y2;
};

struct ColumnSegmentTree {
    int n;
    vector<int> tree;

    ColumnSegmentTree(int size) : n(size) {
        tree.resize(4 * (size + 1), 1e9);
        build(1, 1, size);
    }

    void build(int node, int start, int end) {
        if (start == end) {
            tree[node] = start;
            return;
        }
        int mid = (start + end) / 2;
        build(2 * node, start, mid);
        build(2 * node + 1, mid + 1, end);
        tree[node] = min(tree[2 * node], tree[2 * node + 1]);
    }

    int query(int a, int b, int node, int start, int end) {
        if (b < start || a > end) return 1e9;
        if (a <= start && end <= b) return tree[node];
        int mid = (start + end) / 2;
        int left = query(a, b, 2 * node, start, mid);
        if (left != 1e9) return left;
        return query(a, b, 2 * node + 1, mid + 1, end);
    }

    int find_first_available(int a, int b) {
        return query(a, b, 1, 1, n);
    }

    void update(int pos, int node, int start, int end) {
        if (start == end) {
            tree[node] = 1e9;
            return;
        }
        int mid = (start + end) / 2;
        if (pos <= mid) update(pos, 2 * node, start, mid);
        else update(pos, 2 * node + 1, mid + 1, end);
        tree[node] = min(tree[2 * node], tree[2 * node + 1]);
    }

    void mark_used(int j) {
        update(j, 1, 1, n);
    }
};

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    int n, q;
    cin >> n >> q;
    vector<Rectangle> rects(q);
    for (int i = 0; i < q; ++i) {
        cin >> rects[i].x1 >> rects[i].y1 >> rects[i].x2 >> rects[i].y2;
    }

    ColumnSegmentTree col_tree(n);
    int ans = 0;

    for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) {
        vector<pair<int, int>> forbidden;
        for (const auto& rect : rects) {
            if (rect.x1 <= i && i <= rect.x2) {
                forbidden.emplace_back(rect.y1, rect.y2);
            }
        }
        sort(forbidden.begin(), forbidden.end());

        vector<pair<int, int>> allowed;
        int start = 1;
        for (const auto& [y1, y2] : forbidden) {
            if (y1 > start) {
                allowed.emplace_back(start, y1 - 1);
            }
            start = max(start, y2 + 1);
        }
        if (start <= n) {
            allowed.emplace_back(start, n);
        }

        for (const auto& [a, b] : allowed) {
            int j = col_tree.find_first_available(a, b);
            if (j <= n) {
                ans++;
                col_tree.mark_used(j);
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    cout << ans << '\n';
}<#sep#>-

#define _USE_MATH_DEFINES
#include <iostream>
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <iomanip>

using namespace std;

struct Point {
    double x, y;
    Point(double x_ = 0, double y_ = 0) : x(x_), y(y_) {}
};

double cross(const Point &a, const Point &b) {
    return a.x * b.y - a.y * b.x;
}

double cross(const Point &o, const Point &a, const Point &b) {
    return (a.x - o.x) * (b.y - o.y) - (a.y - o.y) * (b.x - o.x);
}

vector<Point> convex_hull(vector<Point> points) {
    if (points.size() <= 1) return points;
    sort(points.begin(), points.end(), [](const Point &a, const Point &b) {
        return a.x < b.x || (a.x == b.x && a.y < b.y);
    });
    vector<Point> lower, upper;
    for (auto &p : points) {
        while (lower.size() >= 2 && cross(lower[lower.size()-2], lower.back(), p) <= 0) {
            lower.pop_back();
        }
        lower.push_back(p);
    }
    reverse(points.begin(), points.end());
    for (auto &p : points) {
        while (upper.size() >= 2 && cross(upper[upper.size()-2], upper.back(), p) <= 0) {
            upper.pop_back();
        }
        upper.push_back(p);
    }
    lower.pop_back();
    upper.pop_back();
    lower.insert(lower.end(), upper.begin(), upper.end());
    return lower;
}

double area(const vector<Point> &hull) {
    if (hull.size() < 3) return 0;
    double res = 0;
    int n = hull.size();
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        const Point &p = hull[i];
        const Point &q = hull[(i+1)%n];
        res += p.x * q.y - q.x * p.y;
    }
    return fabs(res) / 2.0;
}

vector<Point> original_points;

double compute_area(double theta) {
    double c = cos(theta);
    double s = sin(theta);
    vector<Point> rotated;
    for (const auto &p : original_points) {
        rotated.emplace_back(p.x * c - p.y * s, p.x * s + p.y * c);
    }
    auto hull = convex_hull(rotated);
    return area(hull);
}

int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    original_points.resize(n);
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        double x, y;
        cin >> x >> y;
        original_points[i] = Point(x, y);
    }

    double low = 0.0;
    double high = acos(-1.0);
    const int iterations = 200;
    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
        double mid1 = low + (high - low) / 3;
        double mid2 = high - (high - low) / 3;
        double a1 = compute_area(mid1);
        double a2 = compute_area(mid2);
        if (a1 < a2) {
            high = mid2;
        } else {
            low = mid1;
        }
    }

    double theta = (low + high) / 2;
    cout << fixed << setprecision(3) << theta << endl;

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

struct Edge {
    int u, v, w, idx;
    bool operator<(const Edge& other) const {
        return w < other.w;
    }
};

struct DSU {
    vector<int> parent, rank;
    DSU(int n) : parent(n+1), rank(n+1, 1) {
        iota(parent.begin(), parent.end(), 0);
    }
    int find(int u) {
        return parent[u] == u ? u : parent[u] = find(parent[u]);
    }
    bool unite(int u, int v) {
        u = find(u), v = find(v);
        if (u == v) return false;
        if (rank[u] < rank[v]) swap(u, v);
        parent[v] = u;
        rank[u] += rank[v];
        return true;
    }
};

vector<vector<pair<int, int>>> adj;
vector<int> parent, depth;
vector<vector<int>> up;
vector<vector<int>> max_edge;
const int LOG = 20;

void dfs(int u, int p, int w, int d) {
    parent[u] = p;
    depth[u] = d;
    up[0][u] = p;
    max_edge[0][u] = w;
    for (auto [v, edge_w] : adj[u]) {
        if (v != p) {
            dfs(v, p, edge_w, d+1);
        }
    }
}

void build_binary_lifting(int n) {
    up.resize(LOG, vector<int>(n+1));
    max_edge.resize(LOG, vector<int>(n+1));
    dfs(1, 0, 0, 0);
    for (int k = 1; k < LOG; k++) {
        for (int u = 1; u <= n; u++) {
            up[k][u] = up[k-1][up[k-1][u]];
            max_edge[k][u] = max(max_edge[k-1][u], max_edge[k-1][up[k-1][u]]);
        }
    }
}

int get_max_edge(int u, int v) {
    if (u == v) return 0;
    if (depth[u] < depth[v]) swap(u, v);
    int max_w = 0;
    for (int k = LOG-1; k >= 0; k--) {
        if (depth[u] - (1 << k) >= depth[v]) {
            max_w = max(max_w, max_edge[k][u]);
            u = up[k][u];
        }
    }
    if (u == v) return max_w;
    for (int k = LOG-1; k >= 0; k--) {
        if (up[k][u] != up[k][v]) {
            max_w = max({max_w, max_edge[k][u], max_edge[k][v]});
            u = up[k][u];
            v = up[k][v];
        }
    }
    return max({max_w, max_edge[0][u], max_edge[0][v]});
}

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    int n, m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    vector<Edge> edges(m);
    for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
        cin >> edges[i].u >> edges[i].v >> edges[i].w;
        edges[i].idx = i;
    }

    sort(edges.begin(), edges.end());
    DSU dsu(n);
    adj.resize(n+1);
    vector<bool> in_mst(m, false);
    for (const Edge& e : edges) {
        if (dsu.unite(e.u, e.v)) {
            in_mst[e.idx] = true;
            adj[e.u].emplace_back(e.v, e.w);
            adj[e.v].emplace_back(e.u, e.w);
        }
    }

    parent.resize(n+1);
    depth.resize(n+1);
    build_binary_lifting(n);

    vector<int> max_weight(m);
    for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
        int u = edges[i].u, v = edges[i].v;
        max_weight[i] = get_max_edge(u, v);
    }

    int q;
    cin >> q;
    while (q--) {
        int k;
        cin >> k;
        vector<int> query(k);
        for (int& x : query) {
            cin >> x;
            x--;
        }

        bool possible = true;
        for (int idx : query) {
            if (max_weight[idx] != edges[idx].w) {
                possible = false;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (!possible) {
            cout << "NO\n";
            continue;
        }

        vector<Edge> sorted_query;
        for (int idx : query) {
            sorted_query.push_back(edges[idx]);
        }
        sort(sorted_query.begin(), sorted_query.end());

        DSU q_dsu(n);
        bool has_cycle = false;
        for (int i = 0; i < sorted_query.size();) {
            int current_w = sorted_query[i].w;
            int j = i;
            while (j < sorted_query.size() && sorted_query[j].w == current_w) {
                j++;
            }
            for (int k = i; k < j; k++) {
                int u = sorted_query[k].u;
                int v = sorted_query[k].v;
                if (q_dsu.find(u) == q_dsu.find(v)) {
                    has_cycle = true;
                    break;
                }
                q_dsu.unite(u, v);
            }
            if (has_cycle) break;
            i = j;
        }

        cout << (has_cycle ? "NO" : "YES") << '\n';
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    vector<int> a(n);
    for (auto& x : a) cin >> x;

    vector<int> filled(n, 0);
    int current_book = 1;
    int current_count = 0;
    bool valid = true;

    // Check if existing entries are valid
    int last_val = -1;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        if (a[i] != 0) {
            if (last_val != -1 && (a[i] < last_val || a[i] > last_val + 1)) {
                valid = false;
                break;
            }
            last_val = a[i];
        }
    }
    if (!valid) {
        cout << -1 << endl;
        return 0;
    }

    // Process each day
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        if (a[i] != 0) {
            if (a[i] < current_book) {
                valid = false;
                break;
            }
            if (a[i] > current_book) {
                // Transition to new book
                if (current_count < 2) {
                    valid = false;
                    break;
                }
                int needed = a[i] - current_book;
                int start = i - current_count;
                int available = i - start - current_count;
                int required_days = needed * 2;
                if (available < required_days) {
                    valid = false;
                    break;
                }
                // Fill intermediate books
                int pos = start + current_count;
                for (int j = current_book + 1; j < a[i]; ++j) {
                    for (int k = 0; k < 2; ++k) {
                        if (pos >= i) {
                            valid = false;
                            break;
                        }
                        filled[pos++] = j;
                    }
                }
                while (pos < i) filled[pos++] = a[i] - 1;
                current_book = a[i];
                current_count = 1;
            } else {
                current_count++;
                if (current_count > 5) {
                    valid = false;
                    break;
                }
            }
            filled[i] = current_book;
        } else {
            filled[i] = current_book;
            current_count++;
            if (current_count > 5) {
                current_book++;
                current_count = 1;
            }
        }
    }

    // Check if last book has at least 2 days
    if (current_count < 2) valid = false;

    // Verify existing entries
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        if (a[i] != 0 && a[i] != filled[i]) {
            valid = false;
            break;
        }
    }

    if (!valid) {
        cout << -1 << endl;
        return 0;
    }

    cout << current_book << endl;
    for (int x : filled) {
        cout << x << ' ';
    }
    cout << endl;

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

struct Query {
    int l, r, k, idx;
    bool operator<(const Query& other) const {
        int block_size = 300; // Adjust block size based on constraints
        if (l / block_size != other.l / block_size) {
            return l < other.l;
        } else {
            return (l / block_size % 2 == 0) ? (r < other.r) : (r > other.r);
        }
    }
};

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(NULL);

    int n, m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    vector<int> a(n + 1); // 1-based indexing
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) {
        cin >> a[i];
    }

    vector<Query> queries;
    vector<int> answers;
    vector<pair<int, int>> updates;
    int answer_idx = 0;

    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        int type;
        cin >> type;
        if (type == 1) {
            int l, r, k;
            cin >> l >> r >> k;
            queries.push_back({l, r, k, answer_idx++});
        } else {
            int p, x;
            cin >> p >> x;
            updates.emplace_back(p, x);
            answers.push_back(-2); // Placeholder for update queries
        }
    }

    // Process Mo's algorithm for type 1 queries
    sort(queries.begin(), queries.end());

    int current_l = 1, current_r = 0;
    unordered_map<int, int> freq;
    multiset<int> sorted_counts;
    unordered_map<int, int> cnt_freq; // Frequency of each count
    int max_cnt_freq = 0;

    auto add = [&](int pos) {
        int x = a[pos];
        if (freq[x] > 0) {
            auto it = sorted_counts.find(freq[x]);
            if (it != sorted_counts.end()) {
                sorted_counts.erase(it);
            }
            cnt_freq[freq[x]]--;
            if (cnt_freq[freq[x]] == 0) {
                cnt_freq.erase(freq[x]);
            }
        }
        freq[x]++;
        sorted_counts.insert(freq[x]);
        cnt_freq[freq[x]]++;
        if (cnt_freq[freq[x]] > max_cnt_freq) {
            max_cnt_freq = cnt_freq[freq[x]];
        }
    };

    auto remove = [&](int pos) {
        int x = a[pos];
        auto it = sorted_counts.find(freq[x]);
        if (it != sorted_counts.end()) {
            sorted_counts.erase(it);
        }
        cnt_freq[freq[x]]--;
        if (cnt_freq[freq[x]] == 0) {
            cnt_freq.erase(freq[x]);
        }
        freq[x]--;
        if (freq[x] > 0) {
            sorted_counts.insert(freq[x]);
            cnt_freq[freq[x]]++;
            if (cnt_freq[freq[x]] > max_cnt_freq) {
                max_cnt_freq = cnt_freq[freq[x]];
            }
        }
        // Update max_cnt_freq if the previous max was decremented
        if (freq[x] + 1 == max_cnt_freq && cnt_freq[freq[x] + 1] == 0) {
            max_cnt_freq = 0;
            for (auto& p : cnt_freq) {
                if (p.second > max_cnt_freq) {
                    max_cnt_freq = p.second;
                }
            }
        }
    };

    vector<int> final_answers(answer_idx, -1);

    for (auto& query : queries) {
        int l = query.l, r = query.r, k = query.k, idx = query.idx;
        while (current_l > l) add(--current_l);
        while (current_r < r) add(++current_r);
        while (current_l < l) remove(current_l++);
        while (current_r > r) remove(current_r--);

        int distinct = sorted_counts.size();
        if (distinct < k) {
            final_answers[idx] = -1;
            continue;
        }
        if (max_cnt_freq >= k) {
            final_answers[idx] = 0;
            continue;
        }

        vector<int> counts(sorted_counts.begin(), sorted_counts.end());
        int min_diff = INT_MAX;
        for (int i = 0; i + k - 1 < counts.size(); ++i) {
            min_diff = min(min_diff, counts[i + k - 1] - counts[i]);
        }
        final_answers[idx] = min_diff;
    }

    // Output the answers in the original order
    int type1_idx = 0;
    for (int ans : answers) {
        if (ans == -2) {
            // This is an update query, skip
            continue;
        } else {
            cout << final_answers[type1_idx++] << '\n';
        }
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <iostream>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <memory>
#include <cctype>
#include <sstream>
#include <cassert>

using namespace std;

class Expr {
public:
    virtual int evaluate(int n, const vector<int>& memo) const = 0;
    virtual ~Expr() = default;
};

class NumberExpr : public Expr {
    int value;
public:
    NumberExpr(int v) : value(v) {}
    int evaluate(int, const vector<int>&) const override { return value; }
};

class VariableNExpr : public Expr {
public:
    int evaluate(int n, const vector<int>&) const override { return n; }
};

class SumExpr : public Expr {
    unique_ptr<Expr> left, right;
    char op;
public:
    SumExpr(unique_ptr<Expr> l, unique_ptr<Expr> r, char o) : left(move(l)), right(move(r)), op(o) {}
    int evaluate(int n, const vector<int>& memo) const override {
        int lv = left->evaluate(n, memo);
        int rv = right->evaluate(n, memo);
        if (op == '+') return (lv + rv) % 32768;
        else return (lv - rv + 32768) % 32768;
    }
};

class ProductExpr : public Expr {
    unique_ptr<Expr> left, right;
    char op;
public:
    ProductExpr(unique_ptr<Expr> l, unique_ptr<Expr> r, char o) : left(move(l)), right(move(r)), op(o) {}
    int evaluate(int n, const vector<int>& memo) const override {
        int lv = left->evaluate(n, memo);
        int rv = right->evaluate(n, memo);
        if (op == '*') return (lv * rv) % 32768;
        else return lv / rv;
    }
};

class FunctionCallExpr : public Expr {
    unique_ptr<Expr> arg;
public:
    FunctionCallExpr(unique_ptr<Expr> a) : arg(move(a)) {}
    int evaluate(int n, const vector<int>& memo) const override {
        int k = arg->evaluate(n, memo);
        return memo[k];
    }
};

class Statement {
public:
    virtual int evaluate(int n, const vector<int>& memo) const = 0;
    virtual ~Statement() = default;
};

class ReturnStatement : public Statement {
    unique_ptr<Expr> expr;
public:
    ReturnStatement(unique_ptr<Expr> e) : expr(move(e)) {}
    int evaluate(int n, const vector<int>& memo) const override {
        return expr->evaluate(n, memo);
    }
};

class IfStatement : public Statement {
    unique_ptr<Expr> left, right, retExpr;
    string op;
public:
    IfStatement(unique_ptr<Expr> l, string o, unique_ptr<Expr> r, unique_ptr<Expr> re) 
        : left(move(l)), op(move(o)), right(move(r)), retExpr(move(re)) {}
    int evaluate(int n, const vector<int>& memo) const override {
        int lv = left->evaluate(n, memo);
        int rv = right->evaluate(n, memo);
        bool cond = false;
        if (op == ">") cond = (lv > rv);
        else if (op == "<") cond = (lv < rv);
        else if (op == "==") cond = (lv == rv);
        if (cond) return retExpr->evaluate(n, memo);
        else return -1;
    }
};

vector<string> tokenize(const string& code) {
    vector<string> tokens;
    size_t i = 0;
    while (i < code.size()) {
        while (i < code.size() && isspace(code[i])) i++;
        if (i >= code.size()) break;
        if (isalpha(code[i])) {
            string tok;
            while (i < code.size() && isalnum(code[i])) tok += code[i++];
            tokens.push_back(tok);
        } else if (isdigit(code[i])) {
            string num;
            while (i < code.size() && isdigit(code[i])) num += code[i++];
            tokens.push_back(num);
        } else if (code[i] == '=' && i+1 < code.size() && code[i+1] == '=') {
            tokens.push_back("==");
            i += 2;
        } else {
            tokens.push_back(string(1, code[i++]));
        }
    }
    return tokens;
}

class Parser {
    vector<string> tokens;
    size_t pos;
public:
    Parser(vector<string> t) : tokens(t), pos(0) {}
    
    unique_ptr<Expr> parseArithExpr() { return parseSum(); }
    
    unique_ptr<Expr> parseSum() {
        auto left = parseProduct();
        while (pos < tokens.size()) {
            string t = tokens[pos];
            if (t == "+" || t == "-") {
                pos++;
                auto right = parseProduct();
                left = make_unique<SumExpr>(move(left), move(right), t[0]);
            } else break;
        }
        return left;
    }
    
    unique_ptr<Expr> parseProduct() {
        auto left = parseMultiplier();
        while (pos < tokens.size()) {
            string t = tokens[pos];
            if (t == "*" || t == "/") {
                pos++;
                auto right = parseMultiplier();
                left = make_unique<ProductExpr>(move(left), move(right), t[0]);
            } else break;
        }
        return left;
    }
    
    unique_ptr<Expr> parseMultiplier() {
        string t = tokens[pos++];
        if (t == "n") return make_unique<VariableNExpr>();
        else if (isdigit(t[0])) return make_unique<NumberExpr>(stoi(t));
        else if (t == "f") {
            assert(tokens[pos++] == "(");
            auto arg = parseArithExpr();
            assert(tokens[pos++] == ")");
            return make_unique<FunctionCallExpr>(move(arg));
        }
        cerr << "Unexpected token: " << t << endl;
        exit(1);
    }
    
    vector<unique_ptr<Statement>> parseStatements() {
        vector<unique_ptr<Statement>> stmts;
        while (pos < tokens.size()) {
            string t = tokens[pos];
            if (t == "if") {
                pos++;
                assert(tokens[pos++] == "(");
                auto left = parseArithExpr();
                string op = tokens[pos++];
                auto right = parseArithExpr();
                assert(tokens[pos++] == ")");
                assert(tokens[pos++] == "return");
                auto retExpr = parseArithExpr();
                assert(tokens[pos++] == ";");
                stmts.push_back(make_unique<IfStatement>(move(left), op, move(right), move(retExpr)));
            } else if (t == "return") {
                pos++;
                auto expr = parseArithExpr();
                assert(tokens[pos++] == ";");
                stmts.push_back(make_unique<ReturnStatement>(move(expr)));
            } else {
                cerr << "Unexpected token in statements: " << t << endl;
                exit(1);
            }
        }
        return stmts;
    }
};

int main() {
    int target;
    cin >> target;
    cin.ignore();
    
    string code, line;
    while (getline(cin, line)) code += line + "\n";
    
    size_t start = code.find('{');
    size_t end = code.find('}', start);
    string body = code.substr(start+1, end - start - 1);
    
    auto tokens = tokenize(body);
    Parser parser(tokens);
    auto statements = parser.parseStatements();
    
    vector<int> memo(32768, -1);
    for (int n = 0; n <= 32767; ++n) {
        for (const auto& stmt : statements) {
            int val = stmt->evaluate(n, memo);
            if (val != -1) {
                memo[n] = val;
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    
    for (int n = 32767; n >= 0; --n) {
        if (memo[n] == target) {
            cout << n << endl;
            return 0;
        }
    }
    cout << -1 << endl;
    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;
using ll = long long;

struct Edge {
    int to, rev;
    ll cap, flow;
    Edge(int to, int rev, ll cap) : to(to), rev(rev), cap(cap), flow(0) {}
};

class Dinic {
public:
    vector<vector<Edge>> adj;
    vector<int> level, ptr;

    Dinic(int n) : adj(n), level(n), ptr(n) {}

    void add_edge(int from, int to, ll cap) {
        adj[from].emplace_back(to, adj[to].size(), cap);
        adj[to].emplace_back(from, adj[from].size() - 1, 0);
    }

    bool bfs(int s, int t) {
        fill(level.begin(), level.end(), -1);
        queue<int> q;
        level[s] = 0;
        q.push(s);
        while (!q.empty()) {
            int u = q.front(); q.pop();
            for (auto &e : adj[u]) {
                if (e.cap - e.flow > 0 && level[e.to] == -1) {
                    level[e.to] = level[u] + 1;
                    q.push(e.to);
                }
            }
        }
        return level[t] != -1;
    }

    ll dfs(int u, int t, ll flow) {
        if (u == t) return flow;
        for (int &i = ptr[u]; i < adj[u].size(); ++i) {
            Edge &e = adj[u][i];
            if (e.cap - e.flow > 0 && level[e.to] == level[u] + 1) {
                ll pushed = dfs(e.to, t, min(flow, e.cap - e.flow));
                if (pushed > 0) {
                    e.flow += pushed;
                    adj[e.to][e.rev].flow -= pushed;
                    return pushed;
                }
            }
        }
        return 0;
    }

    ll max_flow(int s, int t) {
        ll flow = 0;
        while (bfs(s, t)) {
            fill(ptr.begin(), ptr.end(), 0);
            while (ll pushed = dfs(s, t, LLONG_MAX)) {
                flow += pushed;
            }
        }
        return flow;
    }
};

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0);

    int n, m, s, t;
    cin >> n >> m >> s >> t;
    s--; t--;

    Dinic dinic(n);
    vector<tuple<int, int, int>> original_edges;

    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        int u, v, g;
        cin >> u >> v >> g;
        u--; v--;
        original_edges.emplace_back(u, v, g);
        ll cap = (g == 1) ? (ll)1e18 - 1 : 0;
        dinic.add_edge(u, v, cap);
    }

    vector<ll> demand(n, 0);
    for (auto &[u, v, g] : original_edges) {
        if (g == 1) {
            demand[u] -= 1;
            demand[v] += 1;
        }
    }

    int new_s = n, new_t = n + 1;
    dinic.adj.resize(n + 2);

    ll total_demand = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        if (demand[i] > 0) {
            dinic.add_edge(new_s, i, demand[i]);
            total_demand += demand[i];
        } else if (demand[i] < 0) {
            dinic.add_edge(i, new_t, -demand[i]);
        }
    }

    dinic.add_edge(t, s, (ll)1e18);

    ll feasible_flow = dinic.max_flow(new_s, new_t);
    ll augmented_flow = dinic.max_flow(s, t);

    vector<ll> flows(m);
    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        auto [u, v, g] = original_edges[i];
        ll lower = (g == 1) ? 1 : 0;
        ll flow = 0;
        for (auto &e : dinic.adj[u]) {
            if (e.to == v && e.cap > 0) {
                flow = e.flow;
                break;
            }
        }
        flows[i] = lower + flow;
    }

    vector<bool> in_S(n + 2, false);
    queue<int> q;
    q.push(s);
    in_S[s] = true;
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.front(); q.pop();
        for (auto &e : dinic.adj[u]) {
            if (e.cap - e.flow > 0 && !in_S[e.to]) {
                in_S[e.to] = true;
                q.push(e.to);
            }
        }
    }

    int k = 0;
    vector<pair<ll, ll>> res(m);
    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        auto [u, v, g] = original_edges[i];
        ll flow = flows[i];
        if (g == 1) {
            bool saturated = (in_S[u] && !in_S[v] && flow > 0);
            if (saturated) {
                res[i] = {flow, flow};
                k++;
            } else {
                res[i] = {flow, flow + 1};
            }
        } else {
            res[i] = {0, 1};
        }
    }

    cout << k << '\n';
    for (auto [f, c] : res) {
        cout << f << ' ' << c << '\n';
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

int main() {
    int N, K;
    cin >> N >> K;
    string P;
    cin >> P;
    string third_line;
    cin >> third_line;
    int m = P.size();
    int third_len = N - m + 1;

    vector<int> required_s;
    for (int s = 0; s < third_len; ++s) {
        if (third_line[s] == '1') {
            required_s.push_back(s);
        }
    }

    vector<char> S(N, 0);
    vector<bool> fixed(N, false);

    // Step 1: Check required positions for conflicts
    for (int s : required_s) {
        for (int j = 0; j < m; ++j) {
            int pos = s + j;
            if (pos >= N) {
                cout << "No solution" << endl;
                return 0;
            }
            if (fixed[pos]) {
                if (S[pos] != P[j]) {
                    cout << "No solution" << endl;
                    return 0;
                }
            } else {
                S[pos] = P[j];
                fixed[pos] = true;
            }
        }
    }

    // Step 2: Check forbidden s's with all fixed positions matching P
    vector<int> forbidden_s_list;
    for (int s = 0; s < third_len; ++s) {
        if (third_line[s] == '0') {
            forbidden_s_list.push_back(s);
        }
    }

    for (int s : forbidden_s_list) {
        bool all_fixed = true;
        for (int j = 0; j < m; ++j) {
            int pos = s + j;
            if (pos >= N || !fixed[pos]) {
                all_fixed = false;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (!all_fixed) continue;

        bool is_p = true;
        for (int j = 0; j < m; ++j) {
            int pos = s + j;
            if (pos >= N || S[pos] != P[j]) {
                is_p = false;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (is_p) {
            cout << "No solution" << endl;
            return 0;
        }
    }

    // Step 3: Initialize S with 'a' for non-fixed positions
    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        if (!fixed[i]) {
            S[i] = 'a';
        }
    }

    // Step 4: Process forbidden s's to prevent P occurrences
    deque<int> q;
    unordered_set<int> in_queue;
    for (int s : forbidden_s_list) {
        q.push_back(s);
        in_queue.insert(s);
    }

    while (!q.empty()) {
        int s = q.front();
        q.pop_front();
        in_queue.erase(s);

        bool is_p = true;
        for (int j = 0; j < m; ++j) {
            int pos = s + j;
            if (pos >= N) {
                is_p = false;
                break;
            }
            char c = fixed[pos] ? S[pos] : 'a';
            if (c != P[j]) {
                is_p = false;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (!is_p) continue;

        bool found = false;
        for (int j = 0; j < m; ++j) {
            int pos = s + j;
            if (pos >= N) continue;
            if (!fixed[pos]) {
                char desired = P[j];
                char new_char = (desired == 'a' && K >= 2) ? 'b' : 'a';
                S[pos] = new_char;
                fixed[pos] = true;

                for (int s_prime : forbidden_s_list) {
                    if (s_prime <= pos && pos <= s_prime + m - 1 && in_queue.find(s_prime) == in_queue.end()) {
                        q.push_back(s_prime);
                        in_queue.insert(s_prime);
                    }
                }

                found = true;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (!found) {
            cout << "No solution" << endl;
            return 0;
        }
    }

    // Final check for any remaining forbidden P occurrences
    for (int s : forbidden_s_list) {
        bool is_p = true;
        for (int j = 0; j < m; ++j) {
            int pos = s + j;
            if (pos >= N || S[pos] != P[j]) {
                is_p = false;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (is_p) {
            cout << "No solution" << endl;
            return 0;
        }
    }

    string result;
    for (char c : S) {
        result += c;
    }
    cout << result << endl;
    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

struct Edge {
    int to, rev;
    int cap;
    Edge(int t, int r, int c) : to(t), rev(r), cap(c) {}
};

class Dinic {
public:
    vector<vector<Edge>> graph;
    vector<int> level;
    vector<int> ptr;

    Dinic(int n) : graph(n), level(n), ptr(n) {}

    void add_edge(int from, int to, int cap) {
        graph[from].emplace_back(to, graph[to].size(), cap);
        graph[to].emplace_back(from, graph[from].size()-1, 0);
    }

    bool bfs(int s, int t) {
        fill(level.begin(), level.end(), -1);
        queue<int> q;
        level[s] = 0;
        q.push(s);
        while (!q.empty()) {
            int u = q.front(); q.pop();
            for (const Edge& e : graph[u]) {
                if (e.cap > 0 && level[e.to] == -1) {
                    level[e.to] = level[u] + 1;
                    q.push(e.to);
                }
            }
        }
        return level[t] != -1;
    }

    int dfs(int u, int t, int flow) {
        if (u == t) return flow;
        for (int& i = ptr[u]; i < graph[u].size(); ++i) {
            Edge& e = graph[u][i];
            if (e.cap > 0 && level[e.to] == level[u] + 1) {
                int pushed = dfs(e.to, t, min(flow, e.cap));
                if (pushed > 0) {
                    e.cap -= pushed;
                    graph[e.to][e.rev].cap += pushed;
                    return pushed;
                }
            }
        }
        return 0;
    }

    int max_flow(int s, int t) {
        int flow = 0;
        while (bfs(s, t)) {
            fill(ptr.begin(), ptr.end(), 0);
            while (int pushed = dfs(s, t, 1e9)) {
                flow += pushed;
            }
        }
        return flow;
    }
};

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    int n, m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    vector<pair<int, int>> edges(m);
    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        cin >> edges[i].first >> edges[i].second;
    }

    int low = 0, high = m, answer_k = m;
    while (low <= high) {
        int mid = (low + high) / 2;
        Dinic dinic(m + n + 2);
        int source = 0;
        int sink = m + n + 1;
        for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
            int u = edges[i].first;
            int v = edges[i].second;
            int e_node = i + 1;
            dinic.add_edge(source, e_node, 1);
            dinic.add_edge(e_node, m + u, 1);
            dinic.add_edge(e_node, m + v, 1);
        }
        for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u) {
            dinic.add_edge(m + u, sink, mid);
        }
        int flow = dinic.max_flow(source, sink);
        if (flow == m) {
            answer_k = mid;
            high = mid - 1;
        } else {
            low = mid + 1;
        }
    }

    Dinic final_dinic(m + n + 2);
    int source = 0;
    int sink = m + n + 1;
    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        int u = edges[i].first;
        int v = edges[i].second;
        int e_node = i + 1;
        final_dinic.add_edge(source, e_node, 1);
        final_dinic.add_edge(e_node, m + u, 1);
        final_dinic.add_edge(e_node, m + v, 1);
    }
    for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u) {
        final_dinic.add_edge(m + u, sink, answer_k);
    }
    final_dinic.max_flow(source, sink);

    vector<pair<int, int>> directed_edges;
    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        int e_node = i + 1;
        const Edge& edge_u = final_dinic.graph[e_node][1];
        const Edge& edge_v = final_dinic.graph[e_node][2];
        if (edge_u.cap == 0) {
            directed_edges.emplace_back(edges[i].first, edges[i].second);
        } else {
            directed_edges.emplace_back(edges[i].second, edges[i].first);
        }
    }

    cout << answer_k << '\n';
    for (const auto& p : directed_edges) {
        cout << p.first << ' ' << p.second << '\n';
    }

    return 0;
