#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

vector<double> build_sparse_table(const vector<int>& arr, function<int(int, int)> func) {
    int n = arr.size();
    int k = log2(n) + 1;
    vector<double> st(n * k);
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        st[i * k] = arr[i];
    }
    for (int j = 1; (1 << j) <= n; j++) {
        for (int i = 0; i + (1 << j) <= n; i++) {
            int mid = i + (1 << (j - 1));
            st[i * k + j] = func(st[i * k + j - 1], st[mid * k + j - 1]);
        }
    }
    return st;
}

int get_max(const vector<double>& st, const vector<int>& log_table, int l, int r, int k) {
    int j = log_table[r - l + 1];
    return max(st[l * k + j], st[(r - (1 << j) + 1) * k + j]);
}

int get_min(const vector<double>& st, const vector<int>& log_table, int l, int r, int k) {
    int j = log_table[r - l + 1];
    return min(st[l * k + j], st[(r - (1 << j) + 1) * k + j]);
}

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    int n, k;
    cin >> n >> k;
    vector<int> v(n), c(n);
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) cin >> v[i];
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) cin >> c[i];

    // Precompute log_table for sparse tables
    vector<int> log_table(n + 1);
    log_table[1] = 0;
    for (int i = 2; i <= n; i++) {
        log_table[i] = log_table[i / 2] + 1;
    }

    // Sparse tables for range max and min
    int k_st = log2(n) + 1;
    vector<double> st_v = build_sparse_table(v, [](int a, int b) { return max(a, b); });
    vector<double> st_c = build_sparse_table(c, [](int a, int b) { return min(a, b); });

    vector<double> best_p(n);
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        int low = i, high = n - 1, r0 = -1;
        while (low <= high) {
            int mid = (low + high) / 2;
            int max_v = get_max(st_v, log_table, i, mid, k_st);
            int min_c = get_min(st_c, log_table, i, mid, k_st);
            if (100 * max_v >= min_c) {
                r0 = mid;
                high = mid - 1;
            } else {
                low = mid + 1;
            }
        }
        if (r0 != -1) {
            int max_v_r0 = get_max(st_v, log_table, i, r0, k_st);
            int min_c_r0 = get_min(st_c, log_table, i, r0, k_st);
            double p_r0 = min(100.0 * max_v_r0, (double)min_c_r0);
            if (r0 == i) {
                best_p[i] = p_r0;
            } else {
                int max_v_prev = get_max(st_v, log_table, i, r0 - 1, k_st);
                int min_c_prev = get_min(st_c, log_table, i, r0 - 1, k_st);
                double p_prev = min(100.0 * max_v_prev, (double)min_c_prev);
                best_p[i] = max(p_r0, p_prev);
            }
        } else {
            int max_v_all = get_max(st_v, log_table, i, n - 1, k_st);
            best_p[i] = 100.0 * max_v_all;
        }
    }

    sort(best_p.begin(), best_p.end());

    vector<double> prefix_log(n + 1, 0.0);
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) {
        prefix_log[i] = prefix_log[i - 1] + log(i);
    }

    double sum = 0.0;
    int m = best_p.size();
    int i = 0;
    while (i < m) {
        int j = i;
        while (j < m && best_p[j] == best_p[i]) {
            j++;
        }
        int l = i, r = j - 1;
        double x = best_p[i];
        int m_total = n - l;
        int m_rest = n - (r + 1);

        double p_total, p_rest;
        if (m_total >= k) {
            if (k == 0) {
                p_total = 1.0;
            } else {
                double log_p_total = (prefix_log[m_total] - prefix_log[m_total - k]) - (prefix_log[n] - prefix_log[n - k]);
                p_total = exp(log_p_total);
            }
        } else {
            p_total = 0.0;
        }

        if (m_rest >= k) {
            if (k == 0) {
                p_rest = 1.0;
            } else {
                double log_p_rest = (prefix_log[m_rest] - prefix_log[m_rest - k]) - (prefix_log[n] - prefix_log[n - k]);
                p_rest = exp(log_p_rest);
            }
        } else {
            p_rest = 0.0;
        }

        sum += x * (p_total - p_rest);
        i = j;
    }

    cout << fixed << setprecision(9) << sum << endl;

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    int n, m, q;
    cin >> n >> m >> q;
    vector<string> grid(n + 1);
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) {
        string s;
        cin >> s;
        grid[i] = " " + s;
    }

    vector<vector<int>> pr(n + 1, vector<int>(m + 1));
    vector<vector<int>> pg(n + 1, vector<int>(m + 1));
    vector<vector<int>> py(n + 1, vector<int>(m + 1));
    vector<vector<int>> pb(n + 1, vector<int>(m + 1));

    for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) {
        for (int j = 1; j <= m; ++j) {
            pr[i][j] = pr[i - 1][j] + pr[i][j - 1] - pr[i - 1][j - 1] + (grid[i][j] == 'R');
            pg[i][j] = pg[i - 1][j] + pg[i][j - 1] - pg[i - 1][j - 1] + (grid[i][j] == 'G');
            py[i][j] = py[i - 1][j] + py[i][j - 1] - py[i - 1][j - 1] + (grid[i][j] == 'Y');
            pb[i][j] = pb[i - 1][j] + pb[i][j - 1] - pb[i - 1][j - 1] + (grid[i][j] == 'B');
        }
    }

    int max_k = min(n, m) / 2;
    vector<vector<vector<int>>> pre_sum(max_k + 1, vector<vector<int>>(n + 1, vector<int>(m + 1)));

    for (int k = 1; k <= max_k; ++k) {
        vector<vector<int>> valid(n + 1, vector<int>(m + 1));
        for (int i = 1; i + 2 * k - 1 <= n; ++i) {
            for (int j = 1; j + 2 * k - 1 <= m; ++j) {
                int r1 = i, c1 = j;
                int r2 = i + k - 1, c2 = j + k - 1;
                int sumR = pr[r2][c2] - pr[r1 - 1][c2] - pr[r2][c1 - 1] + pr[r1 - 1][c1 - 1];

                c1 = j + k, c2 = j + 2 * k - 1;
                int sumG = pg[r2][c2] - pg[r1 - 1][c2] - pg[r2][c1 - 1] + pg[r1 - 1][c1 - 1];

                r1 = i + k, c1 = j;
                r2 = i + 2 * k - 1, c2 = j + k - 1;
                int sumY = py[r2][c2] - py[r1 - 1][c2] - py[r2][c1 - 1] + py[r1 - 1][c1 - 1];

                c1 = j + k;
                c2 = j + 2 * k - 1;
                int sumB = pb[r2][c2] - pb[r1 - 1][c2] - pb[r2][c1 - 1] + pb[r1 - 1][c1 - 1];

                if (sumR == k * k && sumG == k * k && sumY == k * k && sumB == k * k)
                    valid[i][j] = 1;
            }
        }

        for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i)
            for (int j = 1; j <= m; ++j)
                pre_sum[k][i][j] = valid[i][j] + pre_sum[k][i - 1][j] + pre_sum[k][i][j - 1] - pre_sum[k][i - 1][j - 1];
    }

    while (q--) {
        int r1, c1, r2, c2;
        cin >> r1 >> c1 >> r2 >> c2;

        int h = r2 - r1 + 1, w = c2 - c1 + 1;
        int K_max = min({h / 2, w / 2, max_k});

        if (K_max == 0) {
            cout << "0\n";
            continue;
        }

        int low = 1, high = K_max, best_k = 0;
        while (low <= high) {
            int mid = (low + high) / 2;
            int k = mid;
            if (2 * k > h || 2 * k > w) {
                high = mid - 1;
                continue;
            }

            int a = r1, b = r2 - 2 * k + 1;
            int c = c1, d = c2 - 2 * k + 1;
            if (a > b || c > d) {
                high = mid - 1;
                continue;
            }

            int sum = pre_sum[k][b][d] - pre_sum[k][a - 1][d] - pre_sum[k][b][c - 1] + pre_sum[k][a - 1][c - 1];
            if (sum > 0) {
                best_k = mid;
                low = mid + 1;
            } else {
                high = mid - 1;
            }
        }

        cout << best_k * best_k * 4 << "\n";
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

struct PivotRow {
    int column;
    bitset<30> variables;
    bool constant;
};

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    int N, S, D;
    cin >> N >> S >> D;

    vector<bitset<1001>> switches(S);
    for (int i = 0; i < S; ++i) {
        int C_i;
        cin >> C_i;
        for (int j = 0; j < C_i; ++j) {
            int b;
            cin >> b;
            switches[i].set(b - 1);
        }
    }

    while (D--) {
        int T_i;
        cin >> T_i;
        bitset<1001> target;
        for (int j = 0; j < T_i; ++j) {
            int b;
            cin >> b;
            target.set(b - 1);
        }

        vector<bitset<31>> augmented(N);
        for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
            bitset<31> row;
            for (int j = 0; j < S; ++j) {
                if (switches[j].test(i)) {
                    row.set(j);
                }
            }
            row.set(S, target[i]);
            augmented[i] = row;
        }

        vector<PivotRow> pivot_rows;
        int row = 0;
        for (int col = 0; col < S && row < N; ++col) {
            int pivot = -1;
            for (int i = row; i < N; ++i) {
                if (augmented[i].test(col)) {
                    pivot = i;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if (pivot == -1) continue;

            swap(augmented[row], augmented[pivot]);

            for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
                if (i != row && augmented[i].test(col)) {
                    augmented[i] ^= augmented[row];
                }
            }

            bitset<30> vars;
            for (int j = 0; j < S; ++j) vars[j] = augmented[row][j];
            pivot_rows.push_back({col, vars, (bool)augmented[row][S]});

            row++;
        }

        bool inconsistent = false;
        for (int i = row; i < N; ++i) {
            if (augmented[i].test(S)) {
                inconsistent = true;
                break;
            }
        }

        if (inconsistent) {
            cout << "-1\n";
            continue;
        }

        unordered_set<int> pivot_columns;
        for (const auto& pr : pivot_rows) pivot_columns.insert(pr.column);
        vector<int> free_vars;
        for (int j = 0; j < S; ++j) {
            if (!pivot_columns.count(j)) free_vars.push_back(j);
        }

        sort(pivot_rows.begin(), pivot_rows.end(), [](const PivotRow& a, const PivotRow& b) {
            return a.column > b.column;
        });

        int f = free_vars.size();
        int min_switches = S + 1;
        for (int mask = 0; mask < (1 << f); ++mask) {
            bitset<30> x;
            for (int j = 0; j < f; ++j) {
                if (mask & (1 << j)) x.set(free_vars[j]);
            }

            for (const auto& pr : pivot_rows) {
                bool sum = pr.constant;
                for (int k = 0; k < S; ++k) {
                    if (k != pr.column && pr.variables.test(k)) {
                        sum ^= x[k];
                    }
                }
                x[pr.column] = sum;
            }

            min_switches = min(min_switches, (int)x.count());
        }

        if (min_switches > S) cout << "-1\n";
        else cout << min_switches << "\n";
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

using ll = long long;
using pii = pair<int, int>;
const int MOD = 998244353;

struct Vector {
    int x, y;
    Vector() : x(0), y(0) {}
    Vector(int x_, int y_) : x(x_), y(y_) {}
    Vector operator+(const Vector& other) const {
        return Vector(x + other.x, y + other.y);
    }
    Vector& operator+=(const Vector& other) {
        x += other.x;
        y += other.y;
        return *this;
    }
    bool operator==(const Vector& other) const {
        return x == other.x && y == other.y;
    }
};

int cross(const Vector& a, const Vector& b) {
    return a.x * b.y - a.y * b.x;
}

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    int n, m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    vector<Vector> vecs(n);
    for (auto& v : vecs)
        cin >> v.x >> v.y;

    struct State {
        Vector sum;
        Vector last;
        int min_x, max_x;
        int min_y, max_y;
    };

    queue<State> q;
    map<pair<Vector, Vector>, int> visited;

    for (const auto& v : vecs) {
        State initial;
        initial.sum = v;
        initial.last = v;
        initial.min_x = min(0, v.x);
        initial.max_x = max(0, v.x);
        initial.min_y = min(0, v.y);
        initial.max_y = max(0, v.y);
        auto key = make_pair(initial.sum, initial.last);
        if (visited.find(key) == visited.end()) {
            visited[key] = 1;
            q.push(initial);
        }
    }

    int ans = 0;

    while (!q.empty()) {
        State cur = q.front();
        q.pop();

        if (cur.sum == Vector(0, 0)) {
            int dx = cur.max_x - cur.min_x;
            int dy = cur.max_y - cur.min_y;
            if (dx <= m && dy <= m) {
                ans = (ans + 1) % MOD;
            }
        }

        for (const auto& v : vecs) {
            if (cross(cur.last, v) <= 0)
                continue;

            Vector new_sum = cur.sum + v;
            int new_min_x = min(cur.min_x, new_sum.x);
            int new_max_x = max(cur.max_x, new_sum.x);
            int new_min_y = min(cur.min_y, new_sum.y);
            int new_max_y = max(cur.max_y, new_sum.y);

            State next;
            next.sum = new_sum;
            next.last = v;
            next.min_x = new_min_x;
            next.max_x = new_max_x;
            next.min_y = new_min_y;
            next.max_y = new_max_y;

            auto key = make_pair(next.sum, next.last);
            if (visited.find(key) != visited.end())
                continue;

            visited[key] = 1;
            q.push(next);
        }
    }

    cout << ans << endl;

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cstring>

using namespace std;

int main() {
    string s;
    cin >> s;
    int n = s.size();

    // Standard sliding window to compute max_len
    int max_len = 0;
    vector<int> last_occurrence(20, -1);
    int start = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        char c = s[i] - 'a';
        if (last_occurrence[c] >= start) {
            start = last_occurrence[c] + 1;
        }
        last_occurrence[c] = i;
        max_len = max(max_len, i - start + 1);
    }

    // Compute prefix masks and lengths
    vector<int> left_masks(n, 0);
    vector<int> left_length(n, 0);
    last_occurrence.assign(20, -1);
    start = 0;
    int current_mask = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        char c = s[i] - 'a';
        if (last_occurrence[c] >= start) {
            while (start <= last_occurrence[c]) {
                char d = s[start] - 'a';
                current_mask &= ~(1 << d);
                start++;
            }
        }
        current_mask |= (1 << c);
        left_masks[i] = current_mask;
        left_length[i] = i - start + 1;
        last_occurrence[c] = i;
    }

    // Compute suffix masks and lengths
    vector<int> right_masks(n, 0);
    vector<int> right_length(n, 0);
    last_occurrence.assign(20, n);
    int end = n - 1;
    current_mask = 0;
    for (int i = n - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        char c = s[i] - 'a';
        if (last_occurrence[c] <= end) {
            while (end >= last_occurrence[c]) {
                char d = s[end] - 'a';
                current_mask &= ~(1 << d);
                end--;
            }
        }
        current_mask |= (1 << c);
        right_masks[i] = current_mask;
        right_length[i] = end - i + 1;
        last_occurrence[c] = i;
    }

    // Build left_map and right_map
    unordered_map<int, int> left_map;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        int mask = left_masks[i];
        if (left_map.find(mask) == left_map.end() || left_map[mask] < left_length[i]) {
            left_map[mask] = left_length[i];
        }
    }

    unordered_map<int, int> right_map;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        int mask = right_masks[i];
        if (right_map.find(mask) == right_map.end() || right_map[mask] < right_length[i]) {
            right_map[mask] = right_length[i];
        }
    }

    // Precompute max_right
    int max_right[1 << 20] = {0};
    for (auto& p : right_map) {
        int mask = p.first;
        int len = p.second;
        max_right[mask] = max(max_right[mask], len);
    }

    // Dynamic programming to propagate max_right to all subsets
    for (int mask = (1 << 20) - 1; mask >= 0; mask--) {
        for (int bit = 0; bit < 20; bit++) {
            if (mask & (1 << bit)) {
                int sub_mask = mask ^ (1 << bit);
                max_right[sub_mask] = max(max_right[sub_mask], max_right[mask]);
            }
        }
    }

    // Compute the maximum sum of left and right parts
    int max_sum = 0;
    for (auto& p : left_map) {
        int left_mask = p.first;
        int left_len = p.second;
        int allowed_mask = ((1 << 20) - 1) ^ left_mask;
        max_sum = max(max_sum, left_len + max_right[allowed_mask]);
    }

    int result = max(max_len, max_sum);
    cout << result << endl;

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <iostream>
#include <vector>
#include <stack>
using namespace std;

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);
    
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    vector<int> a(n);
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        cin >> a[i];
    }
    
    vector<int> left_max(n, -1);
    stack<int> st;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        while (!st.empty() && a[st.top()] <= a[i]) {
            st.pop();
        }
        if (!st.empty()) {
            left_max[i] = st.top();
        }
        st.push(i);
    }
    
    vector<int> right_max(n, n);
    stack<int> st2;
    for (int i = n - 1; i >= 0; --i) {
        while (!st2.empty() && a[st2.top()] < a[i]) {
            st2.pop();
        }
        if (!st2.empty()) {
            right_max[i] = st2.top();
        }
        st2.push(i);
    }
    
    vector<int> left_subset(n, 0);
    vector<int> last_occurrence(30, -1);
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        int current = a[i];
        unsigned int forbidden_mask = ~current & ((1 << 30) - 1);
        int max_last = -1;
        for (int bit = 0; bit < 30; ++bit) {
            if ((forbidden_mask >> bit) & 1) {
                if (last_occurrence[bit] > max_last) {
                    max_last = last_occurrence[bit];
                }
            }
        }
        left_subset[i] = max_last + 1;
        for (int bit = 0; bit < 30; ++bit) {
            if ((current >> bit) & 1) {
                last_occurrence[bit] = i;
            }
        }
    }
    
    vector<int> right_subset(n, n - 1);
    vector<int> first_occurrence(30, n);
    for (int i = n - 1; i >= 0; --i) {
        int current = a[i];
        unsigned int forbidden_mask = ~current & ((1 << 30) - 1);
        int min_first = n;
        for (int bit = 0; bit < 30; ++bit) {
            if ((forbidden_mask >> bit) & 1) {
                if (first_occurrence[bit] < min_first) {
                    min_first = first_occurrence[bit];
                }
            }
        }
        right_subset[i] = (min_first != n) ? (min_first - 1) : (n - 1);
        for (int bit = 0; bit < 30; ++bit) {
            if ((current >> bit) & 1) {
                first_occurrence[bit] = i;
            }
        }
    }
    
    long long invalid = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        int L = left_max[i] + 1;
        int R = right_max[i] - 1;
        int A = max(L, left_subset[i]);
        int B = min(R, right_subset[i]);
        if (A > B || i < A || i > B) {
            continue;
        }
        long long cnt = (i - A + 1LL) * (B - i + 1LL) - 1;
        invalid += cnt;
    }
    
    long long total = n * (n - 1LL) / 2;
    cout << total - invalid << endl;
    
    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>
#include <stack>
#include <tuple>
#include <cstdio>
using namespace std;

const int MAXN = 500010;

vector<int> adj[MAXN];
int in_time[MAXN], out_time[MAXN];
int parent[MAXN], depth[MAXN], size[MAXN], heavy[MAXN], head[MAXN], pos[MAXN];
int current_pos;

struct SegmentTree {
    vector<int> tree;
    vector<int> lazy;
    int n;

    SegmentTree(int size) {
        n = 1;
        while (n < size) n <<= 1;
        tree.assign(2 * n, 0);
        lazy.assign(2 * n, 0);
    }

    void push(int node, int l, int r) {
        if (lazy[node] == 0) return;
        tree[node] = max(tree[node], lazy[node]);
        if (l != r) {
            lazy[2*node] = max(lazy[2*node], lazy[node]);
            lazy[2*node+1] = max(lazy[2*node+1], lazy[node]);
        }
        lazy[node] = 0;
    }

    void update_range(int a, int b, int val, int node, int l, int r) {
        push(node, l, r);
        if (a > r || b < l) return;
        if (a <= l && r <= b) {
            lazy[node] = max(lazy[node], val);
            push(node, l, r);
            return;
        }
        int mid = (l + r) / 2;
        update_range(a, b, val, 2*node, l, mid);
        update_range(a, b, val, 2*node+1, mid+1, r);
        tree[node] = max(tree[2*node], tree[2*node+1]);
    }

    void update(int a, int b, int val) {
        update_range(a, b, val, 1, 0, n-1);
    }

    int query_point(int idx, int node, int l, int r) {
        push(node, l, r);
        if (l == r) {
            return tree[node];
        }
        int mid = (l + r) / 2;
        if (idx <= mid) {
            return query_point(idx, 2*node, l, mid);
        } else {
            return query_point(idx, 2*node+1, mid+1, r);
        }
    }

    int query(int idx) {
        return query_point(idx, 1, 0, n-1);
    }
};

void dfs_in_out() {
    stack<pair<int, int>> st;
    st.push({1, -1});
    vector<bool> visited(MAXN, false);
    int dfs_time = 0;
    while (!st.empty()) {
        auto [u, p] = st.top();
        st.pop();
        if (visited[u]) {
            out_time[u] = dfs_time - 1;
            continue;
        }
        visited[u] = true;
        in_time[u] = dfs_time++;
        st.push({u, p});
        for (auto it = adj[u].rbegin(); it != adj[u].rend(); ++it) {
            int v = *it;
            if (v != p) {
                st.push({v, u});
            }
        }
    }
}

void compute_size() {
    stack<int> post_st;
    vector<bool> visited(MAXN, false);
    post_st.push(1);
    vector<int> order;

    while (!post_st.empty()) {
        int u = post_st.top();
        if (!visited[u]) {
            visited[u] = true;
            for (auto it = adj[u].rbegin(); it != adj[u].rend(); ++it) {
                int v = *it;
                if (v != parent[u]) {
                    post_st.push(v);
                }
            }
        } else {
            post_st.pop();
            order.push_back(u);
            size[u] = 1;
            for (int v : adj[u]) {
                if (v != parent[u]) {
                    size[u] += size[v];
                }
            }
        }
    }

    for (int u : order) {
        int max_size = 0;
        heavy[u] = -1;
        for (int v : adj[u]) {
            if (v != parent[u] && size[v] > max_size) {
                max_size = size[v];
                heavy[u] = v;
            }
        }
    }
}

void decompose() {
    stack<tuple<int, int, bool>> st;
    st.push({1, 1, false});
    current_pos = 0;

    while (!st.empty()) {
        auto [u, h, processed] = st.top();
        st.pop();

        if (!processed) {
            head[u] = h;
            pos[u] = current_pos++;
            st.push({u, h, true});
            if (heavy[u] != -1) {
                st.push({heavy[u], h, false});
            }
            for (int v : adj[u]) {
                if (v != parent[u] && v != heavy[u]) {
                    st.push({v, v, false});
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

int main() {
    int n;
    scanf("%d", &n);
    for (int i = 0; i < n-1; ++i) {
        int a, b;
        scanf("%d %d", &a, &b);
        adj[a].push_back(b);
        adj[b].push_back(a);
    }

    stack<pair<int, int>> st_p;
    st_p.push({1, -1});
    parent[1] = -1;
    depth[1] = 0;
    while (!st_p.empty()) {
        auto [u, p] = st_p.top();
        st_p.pop();
        for (int v : adj[u]) {
            if (v != p) {
                parent[v] = u;
                depth[v] = depth[u] + 1;
                st_p.push({v, u});
            }
        }
    }

    dfs_in_out();
    compute_size();
    decompose();

    SegmentTree fill_st(n), empty_st(n);

    int q;
    scanf("%d", &q);
    int current_time = 0;
    while (q--) {
        int c, v;
        scanf("%d %d", &c, &v);
        if (c == 1) {
            ++current_time;
            int l = in_time[v];
            int r = out_time[v];
            fill_st.update(l, r, current_time);
        } else if (c == 2) {
            ++current_time;
            int u = v;
            while (u != -1) {
                int h = head[u];
                int l = pos[h];
                int r = pos[u];
                empty_st.update(l, r, current_time);
                u = parent[h];
            }
        } else {
            int ft = fill_st.query(in_time[v]);
            int et = empty_st.query(pos[v]);
            printf("%d\n", ft > et ? 1 : 0);
        }
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

vector<vector<pair<int, int>>> adj; // adjacency list: {neighbor, edge index}
vector<int> edge_type; // current type of each edge (0 or 1)
vector<int> in_time, out_time, depth, parity;
vector<int> parent_edge; // parent edge index for each node
int dfs_time = 0;

vector<int> flip_count; // For Euler Tour segment tree with lazy propagation

void dfs(int u, int p, int current_parity) {
    in_time[u] = ++dfs_time;
    parity[u] = current_parity;
    for (auto &[v, e] : adj[u]) {
        if (v != p) {
            parent_edge[v] = e;
            depth[v] = depth[u] + 1;
            dfs(v, u, current_parity ^ edge_type[e]);
        }
    }
    out_time[u] = dfs_time;
}

// Segment tree for range XOR (flip) and point queries
struct SegTree {
    int n;
    vector<int> tree, lazy;

    SegTree(int size) : n(size), tree(4 * n), lazy(4 * n) {}

    void push(int node, int l, int r) {
        if (lazy[node]) {
            tree[node] = (r - l + 1) - tree[node];
            if (l != r) {
                lazy[2 * node] ^= 1;
                lazy[2 * node + 1] ^= 1;
            }
            lazy[node] = 0;
        }
    }

    void update_range(int node, int l, int r, int ul, int ur) {
        push(node, l, r);
        if (ur < l || ul > r) return;
        if (ul <= l && r <= ur) {
            lazy[node] ^= 1;
            push(node, l, r);
            return;
        }
        int mid = (l + r) / 2;
        update_range(2 * node, l, mid, ul, ur);
        update_range(2 * node + 1, mid + 1, r, ul, ur);
        tree[node] = tree[2 * node] + tree[2 * node + 1];
    }

    int get_parity(int node, int l, int r, int pos) {
        push(node, l, r);
        if (l == r) {
            return (tree[node] % 2);
        }
        int mid = (l + r) / 2;
        if (pos <= mid) return get_parity(2 * node, l, mid, pos);
        else return get_parity(2 * node + 1, mid + 1, r, pos);
    }

    void flip(int l, int r) { update_range(1, 1, n, l, r); }
    int get(int pos) { return get_parity(1, 1, n, pos); }
};

pair<int, int> bfs(const vector<bool>& valid) {
    int n = valid.size();
    vector<int> dist(n, -1);
    queue<int> q;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        if (valid[i]) {
            q.push(i);
            dist[i] = 0;
            break;
        }
    }
    if (q.empty()) return {-1, -1};
    int max_dist = 0, far_node = q.front();
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.front(); q.pop();
        for (auto &[v, _] : adj[u]) {
            if (valid[v] && dist[v] == -1) {
                dist[v] = dist[u] + 1;
                if (dist[v] > max_dist) {
                    max_dist = dist[v];
                    far_node = v;
                }
                q.push(v);
            }
        }
    }
    return {far_node, max_dist};
}

int diameter(const vector<bool>& valid) {
    auto [u, d1] = bfs(valid);
    if (u == -1) return 0;
    vector<int> dist(valid.size(), -1);
    queue<int> q;
    q.push(u);
    dist[u] = 0;
    int max_dist = 0, far_node = u;
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.front(); q.pop();
        for (auto &[v, _] : adj[u]) {
            if (valid[v] && dist[v] == -1) {
                dist[v] = dist[u] + 1;
                if (dist[v] > max_dist) {
                    max_dist = dist[v];
                    far_node = v;
                }
                q.push(v);
            }
        }
    }
    return max_dist;
}

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    adj.resize(n);
    edge_type.resize(n-1);
    parent_edge.resize(n, -1);
    in_time.resize(n);
    out_time.resize(n);
    depth.resize(n);
    parity.resize(n);
    for (int i = 0; i < n-1; ++i) {
        int u, v, t;
        cin >> u >> v >> t;
        u--; v--;
        adj[u].emplace_back(v, i);
        adj[v].emplace_back(u, i);
        edge_type[i] = t;
    }
    dfs(0, -1, 0);
    SegTree seg(n);
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        seg.update_range(1, 1, n, in_time[i], in_time[i]);
        if (parity[i]) seg.flip(in_time[i], in_time[i]);
    }
    int m;
    cin >> m;
    while (m--) {
        int id;
        cin >> id; id--;
        int u = 0, v = 0;
        for (auto &[node, e] : adj[adj[0][0].first]) {
            if (e == id) {
                v = adj[0][0].first;
                break;
            }
        }
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            if (parent_edge[i] == id) {
                v = i;
                break;
            }
        }
        seg.flip(in_time[v], out_time[v]);
        vector<bool> valid_even(n), valid_odd(n);
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            int current_parity = seg.get(in_time[i]);
            valid_even[i] = (current_parity == 0);
            valid_odd[i] = (current_parity == 1);
        }
        int diam_even = diameter(valid_even);
        int diam_odd = diameter(valid_odd);
        cout << max(diam_even, diam_odd) << '\n';
    }
    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

typedef long long ll;
const int MAXN = 2e5 + 5;

int n, m;
vector<pair<int, ll>> adj[MAXN], adj_rev[MAXN];
bool visited[MAXN];
vector<int> order, component;
int comp[MAXN];
ll gcd_comp[MAXN];
vector<int> components_nodes[MAXN];

void dfs1(int u) {
    visited[u] = true;
    for (auto &e : adj[u]) {
        int v = e.first;
        if (!visited[v]) dfs1(v);
    }
    order.push_back(u);
}

void dfs2(int u, int label) {
    comp[u] = label;
    component.push_back(u);
    visited[u] = true;
    for (auto &e : adj_rev[u]) {
        int v = e.first;
        if (!visited[v]) dfs2(v, label);
    }
}

ll gcd(ll a, ll b) {
    while (b) {
        a %= b;
        swap(a, b);
    }
    return a;
}

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        int a, b;
        ll l;
        cin >> a >> b >> l;
        a--; b--;
        adj[a].emplace_back(b, l);
        adj_rev[b].emplace_back(a, l);
    }

    fill(visited, visited + n, false);
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i)
        if (!visited[i])
            dfs1(i);

    fill(visited, visited + n, false);
    reverse(order.begin(), order.end());
    int label = 0;
    for (int u : order) {
        if (!visited[u]) {
            component.clear();
            dfs2(u, label);
            components_nodes[label] = component;
            label++;
        }
    }

    for (int c = 0; c < label; ++c) {
        auto &nodes = components_nodes[c];
        vector<ll> dist(n, -1);
        queue<int> q;
        int start = nodes[0];
        dist[start] = 0;
        q.push(start);
        ll g = 0;

        while (!q.empty()) {
            int u = q.front();
            q.pop();
            for (auto &e : adj[u]) {
                int v = e.first;
                ll l = e.second;
                if (comp[v] != c) continue;
                if (dist[v] == -1) {
                    dist[v] = dist[u] + l;
                    q.push(v);
                } else {
                    ll delta = dist[u] + l - dist[v];
                    g = gcd(g, abs(delta));
                }
            }
        }

        if (nodes.size() == 1) {
            bool has_self_loop = false;
            for (auto &e : adj[nodes[0]]) {
                if (e.first == nodes[0]) {
                    has_self_loop = true;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if (has_self_loop) {
                g = gcd(g, 0LL);
            } else {
                g = 0;
            }
        }

        gcd_comp[c] = g;
    }

    int q;
    cin >> q;
    while (q--) {
        int v, s, t;
        cin >> v >> s >> t;
        v--;
        int c = comp[v];
        ll g = gcd_comp[c];
        if (g == 0) {
            if (s == 0) cout << "YES\n";
            else cout << "NO\n";
            continue;
        }
        ll d = gcd(g, (ll)t);
        if ((t - s) % d == 0) cout << "YES\n";
        else cout << "NO\n";
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

struct TwoSat {
    int n;
    vector<vector<int>> adj;
    vector<int> comp, order, ans;
    vector<bool> vis;
    int time;

    TwoSat(int vars = 0) {
        init(vars);
    }

    void init(int vars) {
        n = vars * 2;
        adj.assign(n, vector<int>());
    }

    void add_clause(int a, int b) {
        adj[a].push_back(b);
    }

    void add_implication(int a, int b) {
        add_clause(a, b);
        add_clause(b^1, a^1);
    }

    void add_or(int a, int b) {
        add_clause(a^1, b);
        add_clause(b^1, a);
    }

    void add_xor(int a, int b) {
        add_or(a, b);
        add_or(a^1, b^1);
    }

    void add_eq(int a, int b) {
        add_implication(a, b);
        add_implication(b, a);
    }

    void dfs(int u) {
        if (vis[u]) return;
        vis[u] = true;
        for (int v : adj[u]) dfs(v);
        order.push_back(u);
    }

    bool solve() {
        vis.assign(n, false);
        order.clear();
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) if (!vis[i]) dfs(i);
        comp.assign(n, -1);
        vis.assign(n, false);
        reverse(order.begin(), order.end());
        int cnt = 0;
        for (int u : order) if (comp[u] == -1) {
            vector<int> stack = {u};
            vis[u] = true;
            while (!stack.empty()) {
                int v = stack.back(); stack.pop_back();
                comp[v] = cnt;
                for (int w : adj[v]) {
                    if (comp[w] == -1) {
                        vis[w] = true;
                        stack.push_back(w);
                    }
                }
            }
            ++cnt;
        }
        ans.assign(n / 2, false);
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i += 2) {
            if (comp[i] == comp[i + 1]) return false;
            ans[i / 2] = comp[i] > comp[i + 1];
        }
        return true;
    }

    vector<bool> get_assignment() {
        return ans;
    }
};

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0);

    int n, p, M, m;
    cin >> n >> p >> M >> m;

    vector<pair<int, int>> complaints;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        int x, y;
        cin >> x >> y;
        --x; --y;
        complaints.emplace_back(x, y);
    }

    vector<pair<int, int>> stations(p);
    for (int i = 0; i < p; ++i) {
        int l, r;
        cin >> l >> r;
        stations[i] = {l, r};
    }

    vector<pair<int, int>> interference(m);
    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        int u, v;
        cin >> u >> v;
        --u; --v;
        interference.emplace_back(u, v);
    }

    int L = 1, U = M;
    for (auto [x, y] : complaints) {
        int min_l = min(stations[x].first, stations[y].first);
        int max_r = max(stations[x].second, stations[y].second);
        L = max(L, min_l);
        U = min(U, max_r);
    }
    if (L > U) {
        cout << "-1\n";
        return 0;
    }

    for (int f = L; f <= U; ++f) {
        vector<bool> available(p);
        bool valid_f = true;
        for (auto [x, y] : complaints) {
            bool x_ok = (stations[x].first <= f && f <= stations[x].second);
            bool y_ok = (stations[y].first <= f && f <= stations[y].second);
            if (!x_ok && !y_ok) {
                valid_f = false;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (!valid_f) continue;

        for (int i = 0; i < p; ++i) {
            available[i] = (stations[i].first <= f && f <= stations[i].second);
        }

        TwoSat ts(p);
        for (auto [x, y] : complaints) {
            if (available[x] && available[y]) {
                ts.add_or(2*x, 2*y);
            } else if (available[x]) {
                ts.add_clause(2*x, 2*x);
            } else if (available[y]) {
                ts.add_clause(2*y, 2*y);
            } else {
                // not possible since f is valid
            }
        }

        for (auto [u, v] : interference) {
            if (available[u] && available[v]) {
                ts.add_or(2*u + 1, 2*v + 1);
            }
        }

        if (ts.solve()) {
            vector<int> selected;
            auto assign = ts.get_assignment();
            for (int i = 0; i < p; ++i) {
                if (assign[i]) {
                    selected.push_back(i + 1);
                }
            }
            cout << selected.size() << " " << f << "\n";
            for (int x : selected) {
                cout << x << " ";
            }
            cout << "\n";
            return 0;
        }
    }

    cout << "-1\n";
    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

struct MergeSortTree {
    vector<vector<int>> tree;
    int n;

    MergeSortTree(const vector<int>& data) {
        n = data.size() - 1;
        tree.resize(4 * (n + 2));
        build(1, 1, n, data);
    }

    void build(int node, int l, int r, const vector<int>& data) {
        if (l == r) {
            tree[node].push_back(data[l]);
            return;
        }
        int mid = (l + r) / 2;
        build(2*node, l, mid, data);
        build(2*node+1, mid+1, r, data);
        merge(tree[2*node].begin(), tree[2*node].end(),
              tree[2*node+1].begin(), tree[2*node+1].end(),
              back_inserter(tree[node]));
    }

    int query(int a, int b, int X, int node, int l, int r) {
        if (a > b || a > r || b < l) return 0;
        if (a <= l && r <= b) {
            return upper_bound(tree[node].begin(), tree[node].end(), X) - tree[node].begin();
        }
        int mid = (l + r)/2;
        return query(a, b, X, 2*node, l, mid) + query(a, b, X, 2*node+1, mid+1, r);
    }

    int query(int a, int b, int X) {
        return query(a, b, X, 1, 1, n);
    }
};

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0);

    int n, q;
    cin >> n >> q;

    vector<int> p(n+1);
    for (int i=1; i<=n; ++i) {
        cin >> p[i];
    }

    MergeSortTree mst(p);

    while (q--) {
        int l, d, r, u;
        cin >> l >> d >> r >> u;

        long long A = ( (l-1LL) * (l-2LL) ) / 2;
        long long B = ( (n - r) * (n - r - 1LL) ) / 2;

        int cnt_below = mst.query(1, n, d-1);
        long long C = ( cnt_below * (cnt_below - 1LL) ) / 2;

        int cnt_leq_u = mst.query(1, n, u);
        long long D = ( (n - cnt_leq_u) * (n - cnt_leq_u - 1LL) ) / 2;

        int cnt_left_below = mst.query(1, l-1, d-1);
        long long A_C = ( cnt_left_below * (cnt_left_below - 1LL) ) / 2;

        int cnt_leq_u_left = mst.query(1, l-1, u);
        int cnt_left_above = (l-1) - cnt_leq_u_left;
        long long A_D = ( cnt_left_above * (cnt_left_above - 1LL) ) / 2;

        int cnt_right_below = mst.query(r+1, n, d-1);
        long long B_C = ( cnt_right_below * (cnt_right_below - 1LL) ) / 2;

        int cnt_leq_u_right = mst.query(r+1, n, u);
        int cnt_right_above = (n - r) - cnt_leq_u_right;
        long long B_D = ( cnt_right_above * (cnt_right_above - 1LL) ) / 2;

        long long total_outside = A + B + C + D - (A_C + A_D + B_C + B_D);
        long long ans = (n * (n - 1LL) / 2) - total_outside;

        cout << ans << '\n';
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

using ll = long long;

struct Node {
    int l, r;
    mutable ll val;
    Node(int l, int r, ll val) : l(l), r(r), val(val) {}
    bool operator<(const Node& other) const { return l < other.l; }
};

set<Node> intervals;

using Iter = set<Node>::iterator;

Iter split(int pos) {
    auto it = intervals.lower_bound(Node(pos, 0, 0));
    if (it != intervals.begin()) {
        --it;
        if (it->r < pos) {
            ++it;
        } else {
            int l = it->l, r = it->r;
            ll v = it->val;
            intervals.erase(it);
            intervals.insert(Node(l, pos - 1, v));
            auto res = intervals.insert(Node(pos, r, v));
            return res.first;
        }
    }
    if (it == intervals.end() || it->l > pos) {
        return it;
    }
    int l = it->l, r = it->r;
    ll v = it->val;
    intervals.erase(it);
    intervals.insert(Node(l, pos - 1, v));
    auto res = intervals.insert(Node(pos, r, v));
    return res.first;
}

ll powmod(ll a, ll b, ll mod) {
    a %= mod;
    ll res = 1;
    while (b) {
        if (b % 2) res = res * a % mod;
        a = a * a % mod;
        b /= 2;
    }
    return res;
}

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    int n, m, vmax;
    ll seed;
    cin >> n >> m >> seed >> vmax;

    auto rnd = [&]() {
        ll ret = seed;
        seed = (seed * 7 + 13) % 1000000007;
        return ret;
    };

    for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) {
        ll a = (rnd() % vmax) + 1;
        intervals.insert(Node(i, i, a));
    }

    while (m--) {
        int op = (rnd() % 4) + 1;
        int l = (rnd() % n) + 1;
        int r = (rnd() % n) + 1;
        if (l > r) swap(l, r);

        int x = -1, y = -1;
        if (op == 3) {
            x = (rnd() % (r - l + 1)) + 1;
        } else {
            x = (rnd() % vmax) + 1;
        }
        if (op == 4) {
            y = (rnd() % vmax) + 1;
        }

        Iter itr, itl;
        switch (op) {
            case 1: {
                itr = split(r + 1);
                itl = split(l);
                for (auto it = itl; it != itr; ++it) {
                    it->val += x;
                }
                break;
            }
            case 2: {
                itr = split(r + 1);
                itl = split(l);
                intervals.erase(itl, itr);
                intervals.insert(Node(l, r, x));
                break;
            }
            case 3: {
                itr = split(r + 1);
                itl = split(l);
                vector<pair<ll, int>> vec;
                for (auto it = itl; it != itr; ++it) {
                    vec.emplace_back(it->val, it->r - it->l + 1);
                }
                sort(vec.begin(), vec.end());
                int cnt = 0;
                for (auto& p : vec) {
                    if (cnt + p.second >= x) {
                        cout << p.first << '\n';
                        break;
                    }
                    cnt += p.second;
                }
                break;
            }
            case 4: {
                itr = split(r + 1);
                itl = split(l);
                ll sum = 0;
                for (auto it = itl; it != itr; ++it) {
                    ll add = powmod(it->val, x, y) * (it->r - it->l + 1) % y;
                    sum = (sum + add) % y;
                }
                cout << sum << '\n';
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>
#include <iostream>
using namespace std;

struct Interval {
    int a, b, idx;
    Interval(int a, int b, int idx) : a(a), b(b), idx(idx) {}
};

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0);
    
    int n, m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    vector<Interval> intervals;
    intervals.reserve(m);
    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        int a, l;
        cin >> a >> l;
        int b = a + l - 1;
        intervals.emplace_back(a, b, i + 1);
    }
    
    sort(intervals.begin(), intervals.end(), [](const Interval& x, const Interval& y) {
        return x.a < y.a;
    });
    
    vector<pair<int, int>> merged;
    for (const Interval& interval : intervals) {
        if (merged.empty()) {
            merged.emplace_back(interval.a, interval.b);
        } else {
            auto& last = merged.back();
            if (interval.a <= last.second + 1) {
                last.second = max(last.second, interval.b);
            } else {
                merged.emplace_back(interval.a, interval.b);
            }
        }
    }
    
    vector<vector<Interval>> groups(merged.size());
    for (const Interval& interval : intervals) {
        int a = interval.a;
        int left = 0, right = merged.size() - 1;
        int pos = 0;
        while (left <= right) {
            int mid = left + (right - left) / 2;
            if (merged[mid].first <= a) {
                pos = mid;
                left = mid + 1;
            } else {
                right = mid - 1;
            }
        }
        groups[pos].push_back(interval);
    }
    
    vector<bool> kept(m + 1, false);
    for (size_t g = 0; g < groups.size(); ++g) {
        const auto& group = groups[g];
        int s = merged[g].first;
        int e = merged[g].second;
        int current_end = s - 1;
        int i = 0;
        const int group_size = group.size();
        while (current_end < e) {
            int max_reach = current_end;
            int best = -1;
            while (i < group_size && group[i].a <= current_end + 1) {
                if (group[i].b > max_reach) {
                    max_reach = group[i].b;
                    best = i;
                }
                ++i;
            }
            if (best == -1) break;
            kept[group[best].idx] = true;
            current_end = max_reach;
        }
    }
    
    vector<int> removed;
    for (int i = 1; i <= m; ++i) {
        if (!kept[i]) {
            removed.push_back(i);
        }
    }
    
    cout << removed.size() << '\n';
    for (size_t i = 0; i < removed.size(); ++i) {
        if (i > 0) cout << ' ';
        cout << removed[i];
    }
    cout << '\n';
    
    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <iostream>
#include <queue>
#include <tuple>
#include <cstring>
using namespace std;

const int MOD = 1e9 + 7;
int comb[51][51];

void precompute_comb() {
    for (int n = 0; n <= 50; n++) {
        comb[n][0] = 1;
        comb[n][n] = 1;
        for (int k = 1; k < n; k++) {
            comb[n][k] = (comb[n-1][k-1] + comb[n-1][k]) % MOD;
        }
    }
}

int main() {
    precompute_comb();
    int n, k;
    cin >> n >> k;
    int cnt50 = 0, cnt100 = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        int w; cin >> w;
        if (w == 50) cnt50++;
        else cnt100++;
    }

    // Check impossible conditions
    if ((cnt50 > 0 && k < 50) || (cnt100 > 0 && k < 100)) {
        cout << "-1\n0\n";
        return 0;
    }

    int steps[51][51][2];
    long long ways[51][51][2];
    memset(steps, -1, sizeof(steps));
    queue<tuple<int, int, int>> q;

    steps[cnt50][cnt100][0] = 0;
    ways[cnt50][cnt100][0] = 1;
    q.emplace(cnt50, cnt100, 0);

    while (!q.empty()) {
        auto [a, b, pos] = q.front();
        q.pop();

        if (a == 0 && b == 0 && pos == 1) {
            cout << steps[a][b][pos] << "\n" << ways[a][b][pos] % MOD << "\n";
            return 0;
        }

        int current_step = steps[a][b][pos];
        long long current_way = ways[a][b][pos];

        if (pos == 0) { // forward trip
            for (int x = 0; x <= a; x++) {
                for (int y = 0; y <= b; y++) {
                    if (x + y == 0) continue;
                    if (50*x + 100*y > k) continue;
                    int new_a = a - x;
                    int new_b = b - y;
                    int new_pos = 1;
                    if (new_a < 0 || new_b < 0) continue;

                    int c_x = comb[a][x];
                    int c_y = comb[b][y];
                    long long total = (1LL * c_x * c_y) % MOD;

                    if (steps[new_a][new_b][new_pos] == -1) {
                        steps[new_a][new_b][new_pos] = current_step + 1;
                        ways[new_a][new_b][new_pos] = (current_way * total) % MOD;
                        q.emplace(new_a, new_b, new_pos);
                    } else if (steps[new_a][new_b][new_pos] == current_step + 1) {
                        ways[new_a][new_b][new_pos] = (ways[new_a][new_b][new_pos] + current_way * total) % MOD;
                    }
                }
            }
        } else { // backward trip
            int available_50 = cnt50 - a;
            int available_100 = cnt100 - b;
            for (int x = 0; x <= available_50; x++) {
                for (int y = 0; y <= available_100; y++) {
                    if (x + y == 0) continue;
                    if (50*x + 100*y > k) continue;
                    int new_a = a + x;
                    int new_b = b + y;
                    int new_pos = 0;
                    if (new_a > cnt50 || new_b > cnt100) continue;

                    int c_x = comb[available_50][x];
                    int c_y = comb[available_100][y];
                    long long total = (1LL * c_x * c_y) % MOD;

                    if (steps[new_a][new_b][new_pos] == -1) {
                        steps[new_a][new_b][new_pos] = current_step + 1;
                        ways[new_a][new_b][new_pos] = (current_way * total) % MOD;
                        q.emplace(new_a, new_b, new_pos);
                    } else if (steps[new_a][new_b][new_pos] == current_step + 1) {
                        ways[new_a][new_b][new_pos] = (ways[new_a][new_b][new_pos] + current_way * total) % MOD;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // If target not reached
    cout << "-1\n0\n";
    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>
#include <tuple>
#include <numeric>
using namespace std;

vector<pair<int, int>> factorize(int n) {
    vector<pair<int, int>> factors;
    for (int i = 2; i * i <= n; ++i) {
        if (n % i == 0) {
            int cnt = 0;
            while (n % i == 0) {
                cnt++;
                n /= i;
            }
            factors.emplace_back(i, cnt);
        }
    }
    if (n > 1) {
        factors.emplace_back(n, 1);
    }
    return factors;
}

bool is_2_type(int d, const vector<pair<int, int>>& b_factors) {
    auto d_factors = factorize(d);
    for (const auto& [p, e] : d_factors) {
        bool found = false;
        for (const auto& [bp, be] : b_factors) {
            if (p == bp) {
                found = true;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (!found) return false;
    }
    return true;
}

bool is_3_type(int d, int b) {
    return (b - 1) % d == 0;
}

bool is_11_type(int d, int b) {
    return (b + 1) % d == 0;
}

int compute_k(int d, const vector<pair<int, int>>& b_factors) {
    auto d_factors = factorize(d);
    int k = 0;
    for (const auto& [p, e] : d_factors) {
        int b_exp = 0;
        for (const auto& [bp, be] : b_factors) {
            if (p == bp) {
                b_exp = be;
                break;
            }
        }
        int k_p = (e + b_exp - 1) / b_exp; // ceil(e / b_exp)
        k = max(k, k_p);
    }
    return k;
}

vector<int> get_factors(int d) {
    vector<int> factors;
    for (int i = 1; i * i <= d; ++i) {
        if (d % i == 0) {
            if (i != 1) factors.push_back(i);
            if (i != d / i && d / i != 1) factors.push_back(d / i);
        }
    }
    sort(factors.begin(), factors.end());
    factors.erase(unique(factors.begin(), factors.end()), factors.end());
    return factors;
}

bool check_six_type(int d, int b, const vector<pair<int, int>>& b_factors) {
    vector<int> factors = get_factors(d);
    for (int f : factors) {
        int m = d / f;
        if (gcd(f, m) != 1) continue;
        bool f2 = is_2_type(f, b_factors);
        bool f3 = is_3_type(f, b);
        bool f11 = is_11_type(f, b);
        if (!f2 && !f3 && !f11) continue;
        bool m2 = is_2_type(m, b_factors);
        bool m3 = is_3_type(m, b);
        bool m11 = is_11_type(m, b);
        if ((f2 && (m3 || m11)) || (f3 && (m2 || m11)) || (f11 && (m2 || m3))) {
            return true;
        }
        if (check_six_type(m, b, b_factors)) {
            return true;
        }
    }
    return false;
}

int main() {
    int b, d;
    scanf("%d %d", &b, &d);
    
    auto b_factors = factorize(b);
    
    // Check 2-type
    if (is_2_type(d, b_factors)) {
        printf("2-type\n");
        printf("%d\n", compute_k(d, b_factors));
        return 0;
    }
    
    // Check 3-type
    if (is_3_type(d, b)) {
        printf("3-type\n");
        return 0;
    }
    
    // Check 11-type
    if (is_11_type(d, b)) {
        printf("11-type\n");
        return 0;
    }
    
    // Check 6-type
    if (check_six_type(d, b, b_factors)) {
        printf("6-type\n");
        return 0;
    }
    
    // Else, 7-type
    printf("7-type\n");
    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

const int INF = 1e9;

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    int n, c;
    cin >> n >> c;

    vector<int> left_min(n + 2, INF);
    vector<int> left_max(n + 2, -INF);
    vector<int> right_min(n + 2, INF);
    vector<int> right_max(n + 2, -INF);

    for (int i = 0; i < c; ++i) {
        int a, b;
        string dir;
        cin >> a >> b >> dir;
        if (dir == "LEFT") {
            if (b < left_min[a]) left_min[a] = b;
            if (b > left_max[a]) left_max[a] = b;
        } else {
            if (b < right_min[a]) right_min[a] = b;
            if (b > right_max[a]) right_max[a] = b;
        }
    }

    vector<int> left_child(n + 2, 0);
    vector<int> right_child(n + 2, 0);
    vector<int> size(n + 2, 0);
    bool impossible = false;

    for (int ai = n; ai >= 1; --ai) {
        bool has_left = (left_min[ai] != INF);
        int current_left_size = 0;

        if (has_left) {
            left_child[ai] = ai + 1;
            if (ai + 1 > n) {
                impossible = true;
            } else {
                current_left_size = size[ai + 1];
                if (left_min[ai] < ai + 1 || left_max[ai] > ai + 1 + current_left_size - 1) {
                    impossible = true;
                }
            }
        }

        bool has_right = (right_min[ai] != INF);
        int current_right_size = 0;

        if (has_right) {
            if (has_left) {
                right_child[ai] = ai + 1 + current_left_size;
            } else {
                right_child[ai] = ai + 1;
            }

            if (right_child[ai] > n) {
                impossible = true;
            } else {
                current_right_size = size[right_child[ai]];
                if (right_min[ai] < right_child[ai] || right_max[ai] > right_child[ai] + current_right_size - 1) {
                    impossible = true;
                }
            }
        }

        size[ai] = 1 + current_left_size + current_right_size;

        if (ai == 1 && size[ai] != n) {
            impossible = true;
        }

        if (impossible) break;
    }

    if (impossible) {
        cout << "IMPOSSIBLE\n";
        return 0;
    }

    vector<int> in_order;
    stack<pair<int, bool>> s;
    s.push({1, false});

    while (!s.empty()) {
        auto [node, visited] = s.top();
        s.pop();

        if (!visited) {
            if (right_child[node] != 0) {
                s.push({right_child[node], false});
            }
            s.push({node, true});
            if (left_child[node] != 0) {
                s.push({left_child[node], false});
            }
        } else {
            in_order.push_back(node);
        }
    }

    for (size_t i = 0; i < in_order.size(); ++i) {
        if (i > 0) cout << " ";
        cout << in_order[i];
    }
    cout << "\n";
    return 0;

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

typedef long long ll;

struct SwapInfo {
    ll s;
    int i, j;
    bool operator<(const SwapInfo& other) const {
        return s < other.s;
    }
};

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0);

    int n, m;
    cin >> n;
    vector<ll> a(n);
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) cin >> a[i];
    cin >> m;
    vector<ll> b(m);
    for (int j = 0; j < m; ++j) cin >> b[j];

    ll sum_a = accumulate(a.begin(), a.end(), 0LL);
    ll sum_b = accumulate(b.begin(), b.end(), 0LL);
    ll D = sum_a - sum_b;
    ll best_v = abs(D);
    vector<pair<int, int>> swaps;

    // Check 0 swaps
    int best_k = 0;

    // Check 1 swap
    vector<SwapInfo> swaps_one;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (int j = 0; j < m; ++j) {
            swaps_one.push_back({a[i] - b[j], i, j});
        }
    }
    ll best_one_v = best_v;
    pair<int, int> best_swap;
    for (auto& si : swaps_one) {
        ll v = abs(D - 2 * si.s);
        if (v < best_one_v) {
            best_one_v = v;
            best_swap = {si.i, si.j};
        }
    }

    if (best_one_v < best_v) {
        best_v = best_one_v;
        best_k = 1;
        swaps = {{best_swap.first + 1, best_swap.second + 1}};
    }

    // Check 2 swaps
    vector<SwapInfo> swaps_two;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (int j = 0; j < m; ++j) {
            swaps_two.push_back({a[i] - b[j], i, j});
        }
    }
    sort(swaps_two.begin(), swaps_two.end());

    ll target_sum = D;
    bool found = false;
    pair<int, int> s1, s2;
    ll best_two_v = best_v;

    for (size_t i = 0; i < swaps_two.size(); ++i) {
        ll s1_val = swaps_two[i].s;
        ll desired = target_sum - s1_val;
        int idx = lower_bound(swaps_two.begin(), swaps_two.end(), SwapInfo{desired, 0, 0}) - swaps_two.begin();

        for (int k = max(0, idx - 2); k < min((int)swaps_two.size(), idx + 3); ++k) {
            if (i == k) continue;
            auto& si_i = swaps_two[i];
            auto& si_k = swaps_two[k];
            ll sum_s = si_i.s + si_k.s;
            ll current_v = abs(D - 2 * sum_s);

            bool same_swap = (si_i.i == si_k.i && si_i.j == si_k.j);
            if (same_swap) continue;

            if (current_v < best_two_v) {
                best_two_v = current_v;
                s1 = {si_i.i + 1, si_i.j + 1};
                s2 = {si_k.i + 1, si_k.j + 1};
                found = true;
            } else if (current_v == best_two_v && !found) {
                s1 = {si_i.i + 1, si_i.j + 1};
                s2 = {si_k.i + 1, si_k.j + 1};
                found = true;
            }
        }
    }

    if (best_two_v < best_v) {
        best_v = best_two_v;
        best_k = 2;
        swaps = {s1, s2};
    }

    // Output
    cout << best_v << '\n' << best_k << '\n';
    for (auto& p : swaps) {
        cout << p.first << ' ' << p.second << '\n';
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <iostream>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <map>
#include <algorithm>

using namespace std;

struct TestCase {
    int n, m, k;
    vector<pair<int, int>> edges;
};

vector<TestCase> read_input() {
    int t;
    cin >> t;
    vector<TestCase> cases(t);
    for (int i = 0; i < t; ++i) {
        cin >> cases[i].n >> cases[i].m >> cases[i].k;
        cases[i].edges.resize(cases[i].m);
        for (int j = 0; j < cases[i].m; ++j) {
            cin >> cases[i].edges[j].first >> cases[i].edges[j].second;
        }
    }
    return cases;
}

vector<int> solve_case(const TestCase &tc) {
    int n = tc.n, m = tc.m, k = tc.k;
    vector<int> degrees(n + 1, 0);
    for (const auto &e : tc.edges) {
        degrees[e.first]++;
        degrees[e.second]++;
    }
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) {
        if (degrees[i] > 2 * k) {
            return vector<int>(m, 0);
        }
    }

    vector<int> edge_company(m, 0);
    vector<unordered_set<int>> city_companies(n + 1);
    map<int, int> company_edges;
    int current_company = 1;

    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        int u = tc.edges[i].first;
        int v = tc.edges[i].second;

        unordered_set<int> candidates_set;
        for (int c : city_companies[u]) {
            if (company_edges[c] < 2) {
                candidates_set.insert(c);
            }
        }
        for (int c : city_companies[v]) {
            if (company_edges[c] < 2) {
                candidates_set.insert(c);
            }
        }
        vector<int> candidates(candidates_set.begin(), candidates_set.end());

        sort(candidates.begin(), candidates.end(), [&](int a, int b) {
            bool a_in_u = city_companies[u].count(a);
            bool a_in_v = city_companies[v].count(a);
            bool b_in_u = city_companies[u].count(b);
            bool b_in_v = city_companies[v].count(b);
            int a_score = (a_in_u && a_in_v) ? 2 : (a_in_u || a_in_v) ? 1 : 0;
            int b_score = (b_in_u && b_in_v) ? 2 : (b_in_u || b_in_v) ? 1 : 0;
            return a_score > b_score;
        });

        bool assigned = false;
        for (int c : candidates) {
            bool in_u = city_companies[u].count(c);
            bool in_v = city_companies[v].count(c);
            if (in_u && in_v) {
                edge_company[i] = c;
                company_edges[c]++;
                assigned = true;
                break;
            } else if (in_u) {
                if (city_companies[v].size() < k) {
                    edge_company[i] = c;
                    company_edges[c]++;
                    city_companies[v].insert(c);
                    assigned = true;
                    break;
                }
            } else if (in_v) {
                if (city_companies[u].size() < k) {
                    edge_company[i] = c;
                    company_edges[c]++;
                    city_companies[u].insert(c);
                    assigned = true;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        if (!assigned) {
            if (city_companies[u].size() < k && city_companies[v].size() < k) {
                edge_company[i] = current_company;
                company_edges[current_company] = 1;
                city_companies[u].insert(current_company);
                city_companies[v].insert(current_company);
                current_company++;
                assigned = true;
            } else {
                return vector<int>(m, 0);
            }
        }
    }

    return edge_company;
}

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    vector<TestCase> cases = read_input();
    for (const auto &tc : cases) {
        vector<int> res = solve_case(tc);
        for (int i = 0; i < res.size(); ++i) {
            cout << res[i] << (i == res.size() - 1 ? "\n" : " ");
        }
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>
#include <iostream>
#include <cmath>
#include <iomanip>

using namespace std;

struct Segment {
    double s, e;
    double m, b; // y = m*(x - a) + b

    Segment(double s_, double e_, double m_, double b_) : s(s_), e(e_), m(m_), b(b_) {}
};

vector<double> compute_visible_areas(int n, int k, const vector<vector<int>>& y) {
    vector<double> res(n, 0.0);
    for (int a = 0; a < k; ++a) {
        vector<Segment> upper;
        for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double b_j = y[j][a];
            double m_j = y[j][a+1] - b_j;
            if (upper.empty()) {
                double area = 0.5 * (b_j + (m_j * 1 + b_j)) * 1.0;
                res[j] += area;
                upper.emplace_back(a, a+1, m_j, b_j);
            } else {
                vector<double> critical = { (double)a, (double)a+1 };
                for (const auto& seg : upper) {
                    double denom = m_j - seg.m;
                    if (denom == 0) {
                        if (b_j > seg.b) {
                            critical.push_back(seg.s);
                            critical.push_back(seg.e);
                        }
                    } else {
                        double x = a + (seg.b - b_j) / denom;
                        if (x >= seg.s && x <= seg.e) {
                            critical.push_back(x);
                        }
                    }
                }
                sort(critical.begin(), critical.end());
                critical.erase(unique(critical.begin(), critical.end()), critical.end());
                double visible = 0.0;
                for (int i = 0; i < (int)critical.size()-1; ++i) {
                    double x0 = critical[i], x1 = critical[i+1];
                    x0 = max(x0, (double)a);
                    x1 = min(x1, (double)a+1);
                    if (x0 >= x1) continue;
                    double mid = (x0 + x1) / 2;
                    double y_j = m_j * (mid - a) + b_j;
                    double y_upper = 0.0;
                    bool found = false;
                    for (const auto& seg : upper) {
                        if (seg.s <= mid && seg.e >= mid) {
                            y_upper = seg.m * (mid - a) + seg.b;
                            found = true;
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                    if (!found) continue;
                    if (y_j > y_upper) {
                        double m_u = 0, b_u = 0;
                        for (const auto& seg : upper) {
                            if (seg.s <= mid && seg.e >= mid) {
                                m_u = seg.m;
                                b_u = seg.b;
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                        double yj0 = m_j*(x0 -a) + b_j;
                        double yj1 = m_j*(x1 -a) + b_j;
                        double yu0 = m_u*(x0 -a) + b_u;
                        double yu1 = m_u*(x1 -a) + b_u;
                        visible += 0.5 * (yj0 + yj1 - yu0 - yu1) * (x1 - x0);
                    }
                }
                res[j] += visible;

                vector<Segment> new_upper;
                for (int i = 0; i < (int)critical.size()-1; ++i) {
                    double x0 = critical[i], x1 = critical[i+1];
                    x0 = max(x0, (double)a);
                    x1 = min(x1, (double)a+1);
                    if (x0 >= x1) continue;
                    double mid = (x0 + x1)/2;
                    double y_j = m_j*(mid -a) + b_j;
                    double y_upper = 0.0;
                    bool found = false;
                    for (const auto& seg : upper) {
                        if (seg.s <= mid && seg.e >= mid) {
                            y_upper = seg.m*(mid -a) + seg.b;
                            found = true;
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                    if (found && y_j > y_upper) {
                        new_upper.emplace_back(x0, x1, m_j, b_j);
                    } else {
                        for (const auto& seg : upper) {
                            double s = max(seg.s, x0);
                            double e = min(seg.e, x1);
                            if (s < e) {
                                new_upper.emplace_back(s, e, seg.m, seg.b);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                upper.clear();
                for (const auto& seg : new_upper) {
                    if (!upper.empty() && upper.back().e == seg.s && upper.back().m == seg.m && upper.back().b == seg.b) {
                        upper.back().e = seg.e;
                    } else {
                        upper.push_back(seg);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    return res;
}

int main() {
    int n, k;
    cin >> n >> k;
    vector<vector<int>> y(n, vector<int>(k+1));
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (int j = 0; j <= k; ++j) {
            cin >> y[i][j];
        }
    }
    vector<double> res = compute_visible_areas(n, k, y);
    cout << fixed << setprecision(12);
    for (double r : res) {
        cout << r << '\n';
    }
    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

struct Node {
    int start, end;
    long long sum;
    int color;
    int left = -1, right = -1;
};

vector<Node> tree;

int create_node(int start, int end) {
    Node node;
    node.start = start;
    node.end = end;
    node.sum = 0;
    if (start == end) {
        node.color = start;
    } else {
        node.color = -1;
    }
    tree.push_back(node);
    return tree.size() - 1;
}

void build(int node) {
    Node &current = tree[node];
    if (current.start == current.end) {
        return;
    }
    int mid = (current.start + current.end) / 2;
    int left = create_node(current.start, mid);
    int right = create_node(mid + 1, current.end);
    current.left = left;
    current.right = right;
    build(left);
    build(right);
    current.sum = tree[left].sum + tree[right].sum;
    if (tree[left].color != -1 && tree[right].color != -1 && tree[left].color == tree[right].color) {
        current.color = tree[left].color;
    } else {
        current.color = -1;
    }
}

void update(int node, int l, int r, int x) {
    Node &current = tree[node];
    if (current.end < l || current.start > r) {
        return;
    }
    if (l <= current.start && current.end <= r) {
        if (current.color != -1) {
            current.sum += abs(x - current.color) * (current.end - current.start + 1LL);
            current.color = x;
            return;
        } else {
            update(current.left, l, r, x);
            update(current.right, l, r, x);
            current.sum = tree[current.left].sum + tree[current.right].sum;
            if (tree[current.left].color != -1 && tree[current.right].color != -1 && tree[current.left].color == tree[current.right].color) {
                current.color = tree[current.left].color;
            } else {
                current.color = -1;
            }
        }
    } else {
        if (current.color != -1) {
            update(current.left, l, r, x);
            update(current.right, l, r, x);
            current.sum = tree[current.left].sum + tree[current.right].sum;
            if (tree[current.left].color != -1 && tree[current.right].color != -1 && tree[current.left].color == tree[current.right].color) {
                current.color = tree[current.left].color;
            } else {
                current.color = -1;
            }
        } else {
            update(current.left, l, r, x);
            update(current.right, l, r, x);
            current.sum = tree[current.left].sum + tree[current.right].sum;
        }
    }
}

long long query(int node, int l, int r) {
    Node &current = tree[node];
    if (current.end < l || current.start > r) {
        return 0;
    }
    if (l <= current.start && current.end <= r) {
        return current.sum;
    }
    return query(current.left, l, r) + query(current.right, l, r);
}

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0);
    int n, m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    tree.reserve(4 * n);
    int root = create_node(1, n);
    build(root);
    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        int type;

        if (type == 1) {
            int l, r, x;
            cin >> l >> r >> x;
            update(root, l, r, x);
        } else {
            int l, r;
            cin >> l >> r;
            cout << query(root, l, r) << '\n';
        }
    }
    return 0;
